## Scope and question

**Question.** Re-derive M1 so that the tool replaces ACMELIFT's rigid S&OP mechanics (Infor dEPM batch jobs, copy-forward instances P/R/A/C, Excel back-allocation) with **Kinaxis-style concurrent planning**: a planner changes a number and immediately sees the consequence on the whole plan — Sales / Invoices → Requested production → plant × manufacturing-key load, confirmation and capacity → projected inventory — inside a scenario they created, which they can compare with the base and confirm without losing the base or traceability; a confirmed change by sales is visible to operations at once, and the reverse. This is the core value in `.planning/PROJECT.md` [project]. Tell GSD what to build, in vertical slices.

**Fixed by the user (not reopened).**
1. Reframe approved. Forecast accuracy (harness, WMAPE/bias, statistical forecast) is a **quality control** proving the engine and the forecast work: off the critical path, it does **not** block closing M1. Evidence, measures and targets are kept; gates become reported checks [user].
2. About 8 **vertical slices**, each a capability the user sees working end-to-end; slice 1 is a walking skeleton of the whole loop on real data [user].
3. Perimeter = **all data** from slice 1: no reduced perimeter (no subset of entities, models or plants); the bulk engine at real demand grain is in slice 1 [user]. (The user's "no pilot" here was about perimeter; "pilot" in decision 9 is the usage mode.)
4. **User-created scenarios are in M1** [gate2 OQ-10]. They are personal and live only in the tool until confirmed. What leaves the tool is the monthly published official version, under the official naming `CYCLE MM YYYY R` (ops side `C`) [gate2r2 OQ-A; gate2r7; gate2r9].
5. Gate-1 decisions stand and now apply to the accuracy control: G1 unbiased, no margin; G2 binding lags 3-6; G3 AC21/AC22 in M1; G4 C0 = 2022-01; G5 actuals on the 3rd working day [gate].
6. Phases 1-4 keep their numbers and are assets.
7. **Replacing Infor dEPM, Kinaxis / o9-style, is the destination, not M1.** M1 runs next to dEPM with **one writer at a time and many authenticated readers** [gate2r10], and produces a handoff file for dEPM (AC24); simultaneous multi-writer input, dEPM retirement, system of record, in-place promotion and the web app are M2 [gate2r2 OQ-E, OQ-H; gate2r10]. The vendors' principles are the reference; the mechanisms are ours to design [gate2r4]. M1 choices must not block that path (T10).
8. **The DWH is out of the operational logic.** The tool's operational data are the planner inputs, reaching it through three channels: entry in the tool as in today's dEPM masks, import of ERP system extractions, a direct link to Snowflake [gate2r6]. The DWH (Azure SQL `dmsnop` views and the Power BI models on them) serves only to train / calibrate the engine and to verify its effectiveness [gate2r5]. Data roles: T3.
9. **M1 is a pilot** [gate2r6]: it runs on real history loaded as test / verification fixtures through the input layer; nobody keys data twice beside dEPM; live planner use starts with M2 multi-user. M1 closes on the pilot's checks, not on live operation.
10. **Cross-functional concurrency** [gate2r9]: three visibility levels — personal (only the author), shared for review, confirmed; a confirmed change enters the common plan at once and is visible to sales and operations; personal what-ifs stay private until confirmed. The monthly cycle becomes **one monthly review**: work is continuous, once a month the decision is taken and the official versions `CYCLE MM YYYY R` / `C` are frozen and handed to dEPM; the day windows (sales 1-8, ops 10-15) stop being gates.
11. **Roles and access in M1** [gate2r10]: authenticated login and role-based read scopes enforced server-side already in M1, including three view-only roles — Manager (own plant or own LE), Regional Head (sales and ops of his region), Corporate function (all). Read scopes may also restrict by **Product Division**, as today's Power BI row-level security does [gate2r18 MD-Q-6]; a user with several roles **switches role without signing out** [gate2r18 UX-Q-17]; a **global supply planner** role edits the MK → plant links with the data manager [gate2r18 MD-Q-10]. M1 is a **Windows desktop app**; the web app is the next phase (M2).
12. **Masks** [gate2r11]: the plan horizon is always two fiscal years — the rest of the current FY plus the next FY (24 months in January, 13 in December), not 24 rolling months; the closed months of the current FY are always shown as actuals next to the plan. Invoiced and Order Intake keep today's categories External / Own Region / Group / Total, **all four typed — Total is not calculated** [gate2r13]. The sales planning mask carries an **Order Intake forecast** over the same horizon, like Sales / Invoices and Requested production [gate2r13], counting external orders [gate2r15], entered at a level the data manager sets — product line, product family or planning model [gate2r18 UX-Q-20]. Sales inputs are keyed at the **L-2 node** of the sales region hierarchy [gate2r18 MD-Q-2]. Every input field carries an "i" with its definition. **FY = calendar year** [gate2r12].
13. **Opening inventory, Operations Orderbook, frozen zone, master data** [gate2r12]: the opening inventory keeps its own mask at LE × region × planning model with Financial, IC (may be negative) and Total = Financial + IC; FG held in assembly plants and not yet invoiced enters the inventory calculation. **IC leaves out units still held in an assembly plant; those are reported only as FGNI, and S-06 explains the three fields in an info pop-up** [gate2r13]. The ops planner always sees the latest confirmed Requested production and sees and enters the Operations Orderbook, which is **the sales orders of the assembly plants** [gate2r13]; **no source keeps it today** — the ops cube `OrderBook_units` is a copy of the sales order book [gate2r18, U10] —, so it enters only by ops-planner entry and, later, an ERP extract (U5). The frozen zone stays visible and is set **per MK × plant** [gate2r18 MD-Q-7]; inside it Requested production defaults to the Operations Orderbook with its latest dates, and a manual change is an exception with a reason, flagged on the cockpit agenda. M1 includes a master-data specification (DF-11).
14. **Planning BOM** [gate2r14] (the user's name; never "condensed BOM"): configurable products are planned as PM → MK → option groups of components with rates (e.g. an MK uses Pump 1 in 40 %, Pump 2 in 20 %, Pump 3 in 40 %). M1 computes the **component requirement only** — no component capacity, no supply constraint — in a shape that lets component capacity be added later. Rates at MK × region with a global MK fallback; manual and dated, plus a proposal from the observed mix once configuration data arrive (U1), overrides traced; rates are edited by the data manager **and Operations** [gate2r18 UX-Q-24]; inside the order-book horizon the real order configuration wins. Decided by the orchestrator on the user's behalf: exclusive option groups sum to 100 %, optional attachments carry a standalone attach rate, a single level (MK → components) in M1, and the elective NAM routing (NW-04) becomes an option group whose options carry a routing; after the critic round on 9.8, a group is either a **routing group** (at most one per MK, splits the MK load across plants once) or a **component group** (applied to the load at each plant), and a routing group takes precedence over the master `Plant` attribute [AD orchestrator, gate2r14]. Every MK is configurable and the tool holds a Planning BOM for any MK, by import or by hand [gate2r15]. Storage: the item master of decision 15; the user gets **one read view of the whole chain** PM → MK (weights) → plant → semi-finished (2-month offset) → components (rates) → overrides.
15. **Item master** [gate2r16]: one **ITEM** table (element ID, description, classification {planning model, finished good / MK, semi-finished, component, phantom — the SAP phantom assembly: in the BOM, not stocked, its components passing through to the parent}, product and manufacturing hierarchies filled by classification, valid from / to) and three dated structures on it — **ITEM × PLANT** (cluster, assembly hours, frozen-zone length, slot capacity and the other MK-specific attributes; replaces the MK → plant list), **ITEM × REGION** (Region Model Assignment, SOP / EOP, phase-in / phase-out, active; replaces the fixed region on the item) and typed **ITEM LINKS** (PM → MK bridge weights for every bridge case, MK → semi-finished with offset, routing group options, MK → component rates by region or global, phantom explosion) — one mechanism for all special cases. PM and MK are two linked items, never one row (YCRANE = 7 MK, 24 PM, 24 links). Plant-suffixed keys (`CIT-11-01-000_Oakridge` / `_Northfield`) are one item with two ITEM × PLANT rows, their dEPM IDs kept as aliases for the handoff file; **plant-suffixed planning models are merged into their base planning model** the same way [gate2r17]. One **cluster** concept — Assembly Cluster in Power BI = Manufacturing Category in INFOR — serves the sales filter and the capacity side [gate2r18 MD-Q-1]. The sales planner filters and sorts by plant and cluster, a PM made in two plants showing under each with its share, totals adding up; S-04 shows the frozen zone per plant (approved).
16. **Regional sourcing** [gate2r17]: a region may source the same model from plants in other regions — LATAM from an EMEA or a LATAM plant, the LATAM plant preferred; APAC from NAM, EMEA or LATAM plants. Held as a dated rule per item × region × plant (allowed plants, priority / preferred plant, optional default share), read by the plant split before the item-wide plant shares. The rule's design is the architect's (NW-08, T13); its defaults are submitted at the gate (OQ-33).
17. **Master data and calendar answers** [gate2r18]: a full calendar in M1 (day, week with the CW53 rule, month, year); FX rates per currency × month entered monthly by the data manager, the value gap shown in local currency and converted to EUR (price table per planning model or Product Class [gate2r19]); the Portfolio Review also covers new models to create and enter into the plan; hour capacity at plant × cluster, capacity parameters and Planning BOM rates co-edited by Operations; colour is a component of type feature; the dEPM "parameters" cube (region assignment, SOP / EOP per model) to be exported as the ITEM × REGION seed (U11).
18. **Section E answers and scope cut** [gate2r19]: see Decided, gate r19. **Tail & Passenger Lifts are out of scope**: the product line `Taillift` of `DSALES_Product` (80 of 996 models in the parameters cube, U11) and any passenger-lift model are excluded from the seed, the masks, the engine and every check; their history stays in the DWH untouched.

**Constraints.** Python + DuckDB; the 187-test suite (`187 passed in 580.09s` [code]) stays as regression; Power BI stays the reporting surface and reads version-name strings [pbi]; real data never in git; determinism: ordered reads, sums in Python, no parallel `SUM … GROUP BY` [project; vault]; dEPM is the system of record in M1 and receives a file (AC24) [corpus; gate2r2 OQ-E]; M1 client = Windows desktop app (AC25) [gate2r10]; fiscal year = calendar year [gate2r12] (the vault assumed it without stating it [vault]).

## Case list (91 cases)

LN = Legal Name, LE = legal entity (`DSALES_Division` = Sales Division → Company Legal Name), PM = planning model, MK = manufacturing key, FY = fiscal year, FGNI = FG (finished goods) in assembly plants not invoiced, OI = Order Intake. Planner input grain = Sales Division × planning model × month inside mask filters Year, Month, Cycle, Region, Product Line (Ops: Plant instead of Division) [inputs §1]. DWH fact grain = month × region × `DSALES_Product` × division (sales) or month × region × plant × product × MK (ops) [pbi; ops tmdl].

### S — Concurrent edit and scenario cases (11)
| # | Case | Evidence |
|---|---|---|

## As-is, with located defects

**A1 Process and calendar.** Prior month end: job 2000 on the 30th/31st; SOP/EOP update by the last working day; Power BI export on calendar day 3 (no later than day 4 noon) [inputs §4]. Days 1-8 demand planning (P, Sales mask); 8-10 Global Demand Validation (P → R); day 10 handover to Operations; 10-15 supply planning (A, Ops mask); day 15 allocation / constraining (job 4000 "distributes the sales demand across the plants"); day 16 Global S&OP Supply Plan Validation, final adjustments by the Global Supply Planner, job 4100 (C) [inputs §4]; the Accountability Matrix lists Global and Regional Supply Planners among ~14 roles [vault]. Job 3000 initialises P from the previous R and R from the current P [gate2 OQ-20]; jobs 3300 "Transfer Actuals" then 3100 "Calculate Inventory & FMS" run before the demand validation [inputs §2]; job 3600 writes Confirmed into Requested for the month just ended (§3.3, live) [inputs §4; gate2 OQ-23]. The DPP timetable lists 20 steps with 21 day values: its step-to-day alignment is **not recoverable** [inputs §4]. Order book: entered by the sales planner in the planning mask "as of last day of the previous month", firm only if registered in the ERP [inputs §3]; shown "by model and month per the last confirmed delivery date" [vault]; "order book by day 3" [gate2 OQ-8] is **not in the vault**. Frozen zone: "a timeframe before production starts (≈ the product lead time) in which no production request can change", extended one month to the right each cycle [vault; inputs §1]; its length per product or plant is not documented [vault]. Inputs are typed or pasted from Excel; no automatic ERP-to-dEPM feed is named [inputs §3]. The user states that ERP system extractions and a Snowflake source also carry these data [gate2r6]; their layouts, grains and access are not in the evidence (U5, U6). Excel files by hand each cycle: BA kits to Northfield / Ganderkesee, installations, final tweaks [inputs §1]. Locks `STAT_02` → `STAT_03` per instance; "Check Actuals_CK" restricts which division may enter which product line [inputs §4]. S&OP horizon = "two full fiscal years" [vault]; fiscal year = calendar year, assumed and not stated in the vault [vault], confirmed by the user [gate2r12]; S&OP plans only third-party (external) invoiced units [vault]; intercompany flows are "identified and netted out", else "intercompany sales would be double-counted" [vault].

**A1b Planner inputs (dEPM masks) [inputs §1].** Sales planner, Update Sales Planning (P, days 1-8): Sales / Invoices, Order Book, Requested Production per Sales Division × planning model × month, 24 months (2 fiscal years), frozen zone on Requested Production. **Order Intake plan [ana050 §3.4; measured r98; measured r981]:** since the ANA-050 enhancement (go-live 25.07.2025) dEPM also holds an Order Intake plan, entered "on 'product category' level, not on sales model", available in the P and R instances; the sales extract holds 75,374 `OrderIntake_Units` rows on P / R cycle versions (P 39,474, R 35,900), the first in cycles 08-09/2025 (none on `CYCLE 07 2025`), 97.2 % of them on "(E)" product members and 2,130 on leaf products; every `OrderIntake_units` row of the extract, plan and actual, is labelled "Order Intake [external]"; the vault's mask description (UM) predates it and does not list it [inputs §1]. Sales Input Actuals (m−1): `Invoiced Units` (External / Own Region / Group), `Order Intake` (same 3; the UM's column descriptions are mislabelled, meaning uncertain), `Orderbook` (5 columns, only Own Region + Group typed; External taken from the planning mask; a Check column = External orderbook beginning of month + order intake − invoiced units), Inventory (Financial, IC) per Division × product line in the UM, then "Import actuals". The UM names no Total column for Invoiced, Order Intake or Inventory. **IC** = units in own financial stock to be invoiced to group (negative) and units in plant stock to be invoiced to the entity (positive) [inputs §2]. Ops planner, Update Operations Planning (A, days 10-15): Confirmed Production Units, Working Days, Cluster Average maximum Capacity per day, per Region × product line × plant (cluster rows) × month; Sales / Invoices, Order Book and Requested Production arrive **read-only "from the Sales Cycle Requested"** [inputs §1]. Ops Input Actuals: Produced Units per plant × region × product line in the UM, with the note "Plants are not reporting their financial inventory... you need to inform the sales entity of all the units produced and not invoiced" [inputs §1]. **Ops cube order book and inventory [ops-extract; ops tmdl; gate2r18]:** the ops fact (Date × `DSALES_Region` × `DPLANT` × `DSALES_Product` × `DMANUFACT_Key` × `DMEASURE_OPS` × Version) holds `OrderBook_units` (461,127 rows, 142 versions, 2021-01..2027-12, no `I` rows) and `Inventory` (2,900,437 rows, 142 versions, 2021-01..2028-12, no `I` rows); the Power BI ops page "Plant Analysis" shows Order Book per Sales Model × month [pbi]. **`OrderBook_units` in the ops cube is a copy of the sales order book** — the user's statement closing U10 [gate2r18] —, the same pattern as `R_Total` and `InvoicedUnits` (DD4): its plant dimension is the sales order book distributed to plants *(inference until the U10 export)*, not plant orders. **No operations order book measure exists in any source** ("we do not have an OpsOrderbook_units; it is one of the pain points" [gate2r18]). **Operations Orderbook:** no document names it; the user defines it as **the sales orders of the assembly plants** [gate2r13] — the order lines a plant holds for units it will build; today no system keeps it for S&OP, so there is no history to load (DS8). In the 2026-08 Supply Plan, "Dummy Sales Division" rows carry `Inventory` on 592 rows with no sales entity, and Sales Demand and Supply Plan inventory totals match for the actual months 01-07.2026 [xlsx]. **User corrections [gate2r8]:** ops actuals are per manufacturing key, which matches the DWH ops actuals (`C_Total` I at plant × MK [baseline 4]); sales actuals are per planning model, which matches the DWH sales facts (`DSALES_Product` → planning model [pbi]). Data manager: Region Model Assignment, SOP/EOP, horizon ("monthly" per year column); Region Model Assignment (yes / no per model × region) and SOP / EOP per model live in a dEPM "parameters" cube [gate2r18 MD-Q-4], not exported (U11). Computed: future inventory, FMS, production units/day, allocation (4000), confirmation (4100), confirmation back to requested (3600).

**A1c FMS in dEPM [fms].** FMS = Forward Months of Supply; sales cube only (`FMSRate_calc`, confirmed variant `FMSRate_conf_calc`), stored at Date × Region × PM × Division × Version, plus product-line aggregates and a Division "-" level. FMS(m) = whole forward months from m+1 until cumulative Sales / Invoices of the same instance reaches month-end inventory, plus the linear fraction in the crossing month; blank sales = 0; inventory ≤ 0 → 0. Roll-ups ("-" over divisions, product line) apply the same formula to summed inventory and summed sales. Computed by job 3300/3100 (budget 6100) or the "Refresh FMS & Inventory" button, never entered. Not determinable from the evidence: the dEPM rule text, the horizon end for uncovered cells (stored values count down from 53, i.e. a far fixed horizon; 19.9 % of division cells uncovered within 30 months), rounding, the 521 `FMSRate_conf_calc` mismatches (1.3 %), and which sales series is used when P and R both exist. Stored `CYCLE 12 2025 P` spans 2025-07..2027-12, 30 months (current FY remainder shown plus two further years). The Power BI `FMS` DAX (cap 12, prior-year substitution for blank months, blank at zero inventory) is a reporting re-implementation, not the dEPM logic.

**A2 Data (history).** Sales fact: month × region × `DSALES_Product` × `DSALES_Division` × measure × version; product carries `Plant`, `ManufactKey`, `Planning Model` [pbi]. Ops fact: month × region × plant × product × MK × measure × version [pbi; ops tmdl]; confirmed production on region / product "-" on ~99 % of rows (B10). Sales P/R 80 cycles (02/2020-09/2026); ops A 69 / C 68; actuals 2021-01..2026-08 [baseline]; **132 Legal Names over 142 company codes** in the `Company` view [md] (an earlier count of 133 [state] is not reproduced; the digest's inference is a `-` / null name counted in a fact extract), 1,659 PMs (the LEs active for planning = those with plan facts [gate2r18 MD-Q-11]), `DSALES_Product` ~1,661 members [state; vault] (1,888 rows in the DWH view incl. 286 "(E)" members, 1,660 in the dEPM export [md]). Plants: 51; the `Region` attribute has 5 distinct values (EMEA 23, NAM 8, APAC 6 among them), filled on 46 plants and null on 5 (OEM, OEM-Delta, CIS External Installation, Dummy Plant, `SEA (E)`) [md]. Sales regions: 92 members joined by ID on the facts; L-2 is the region level (LATAM 30, EMEA 26, APAC 16, CIS 13, NAM 4, `Global` 1, `SPV (E)` 1 members), L-3 sub-region (23), L-4 country (76) [md]; sales inputs are keyed at L-2 [gate2r18 MD-Q-2]. Currencies: the `Company` view carries one currency per company code, 25 distinct [md]; no FX series is in any source read. Ops measures exported: `Production_units_C_Total`, `_R_Total`, `InvoicedUnits`, `working_days` (129,289), `Capacity_avg_max` (3,787,905), `AssemblyHours` (835,640), `AssemblyCapacity_hours` (603,944), `UnitsBAT_calc` (988,689), all equal to model counts [state]; also `OrderBook_units` (461,127) and `Inventory` (2,900,437) [ops-extract], counted but not exported at fact grain. The DWH holds one inventory number per cell: no Financial / IC / FGNI split exists in either model [pbi; ops tmdl]. Product ↔ MK, product ↔ plant and plant ↔ region are text attributes on dimension rows, not relationships, in both Power BI models [md]. **Plant inside key IDs [md; xlsx; vault]:** all 62 CIT keys have the form `CIT-nn-nn-nnn_<Plant>`; at leaf level one supply product splits into plant-specific keys on 13 components (e.g. `Crane-Tipper (S)` → `CIT-11-01-000_Oakridge` and `CIT-11-01-000_Northfield`); 36 planning models carry a plant suffix, no base key carries two different suffixes, and 26 of 29 bases measured exist in supply without the suffix. **`-BA` and `-BR` IDs [md; vault; md-spec]:** the MK dimension holds 28 IDs with a `-BA` and 13 with a `-BR` pattern (regex count on the ID) [md]; the 21 `-BA` and 8 `-BR` planning models were measured in the plan export, where the `-BA` ones have no supply rows [vault]; no source maps the planning models to the MK IDs, and the spec reads the 28 / 13 as CIT keys while the digest counts them separately [md-spec; md]. The product's `Assembly Cluster` attribute has 78 values (null 24.4 %) and the MK's `Manufacturing Category` 81 values (null 2.6 %, `-` 12) [md]; the user states they are one concept, the cluster [gate2r18 MD-Q-1]; which values differ between the two lists, and how the 73 non-line "(E)" capacity keys (A2b) relate to them, is not measured. Cluster names carry a plant or region (e.g. `Hooklifts & Skip Loaders Westvale`, `Hooklifts Lakeside`, `Platforms Italy`) [md] *(inference: a cluster belongs to one plant)*. The vault names no DWH and no use of history for training [inputs §5].

**A2b Capacity keys (history) [measured r7; measured r7b, scripts/capacity_checks.py].** `working_days` sits on the 14 line keys (`Crane (E)` … `PCC (E)`, Sales Model "… Dummy"). MK → line key = `Product Line` + " (E)", L-2 fallback: 3,787,905 of 3,787,905 capacity rows map; 99.74 % find a `working_days` value. Of 87 "(E)" keys in `Capacity_avg_max`, only the 14 line keys are max-rollups (7,566 of 7,566 C groups equal the max of leaf siblings; E/sum median 0.036); the 73 others are leaves with own capacity, 66 with confirmed production (1,960,037 units); the 14 line keys carry 38,519 units booked directly. Values are per key per day (e.g. 0.0167).

**A3 Code (phases 1-4).** RecalcEngine: concurrent recalculation, early cutoff, DAG requested_production → derived_manufacturing_demand → slots_requested → gap_req_conf → value_gap; `ibp/scenario` branch / edit log / overlay / compare / promote / tombstones / conflicts; 40-table schema; synthetic seed with a 6-month horizon; no forecast, accuracy, input layer, API, login or access control [code]; the `value_gap` node (937 seed cells) has no stated price basis or currency [code]; `DIM_DATE` is month grain (120 rows, 2019-01..2028-12) and `TWSC_PAR_CALWEEK` holds 4,257 seed rows in a shape not mapped to the real cube [md]. A scenario is a sparse overlay on a `DVERSIONS` base; promote copies [arch]. Versions are keyed by name: `db/schema.sql:117` `version_name … UNIQUE`; `promote.py:300-301, :334-335` raise `DuplicateVersionError` on an existing name, asserted by `tests/test_scenario_ac13_promotion.py:113`; `db/seed/facts.py:28` builds a dict keyed on `version_name`; `overlay.py:41-44` and `build_db.py:263` select by name [critic r5]; `ibp/scenario/models.py` builds snapshot labels `SNAP_{version_name}_{iso_ts}` [critic r6]; 12 files under `db/`, `ibp/`, `tests/` read `version_name` [code r9.3]. **Item tables [schema; md].** The schema already has one item master: `item` (`schema.sql:42`) with `role` CHECK in (`planning_model`, `manufacturing_key`, `semi_finished`), a `region` column NOT NULL CHECK in (`APAC`, `CIS`, `EMEA`, `LATAM`, `NAM`, `GLOBAL`) — one value per item —, `is_active`, the commercial attributes (division, line, category) null on keys and the manufacturing ones (line, category, family) null on models, and the MK capacity attributes `average_assembly_hours` and `slot_equivalence_factor`; seed 1,661 planning models + 1,346 keys + 3 semi-finished. `item_identifier` (`:75`) keeps dated aliases of type `element_name` / `caption` / `sap_material` / `legacy` (3,668 seed rows). `item_link` (`:239`) keeps typed dated links `produces` / `aggregates` / `splits` / `dependent_demand` with `quota_pct > 0 AND <= 100`, `quota_source`, `offset_months`, a `period` beside `valid_from` / `valid_to`, no region scope and no group (3,516 seed rows: 3,468 / 18 / 12 / 18) — the adopted bridge design brief [vault]. `production_eligibility` (`:259`) lists MK × plant × `region_scope` × `priority` (582 seed rows, `region_scope` random, `valid_to` always null); `routing` (`:273`) holds steps by `variant_key` with `mandatory` (16 seed rows); `bill_of_resource` (`:286`) is keyed MK × `variant_key`; `edge` (`:204`) is a generic typed dated edge table (746 seed rows). Facts: `TSOP_OPS` is keyed `manufact_key` × `plant_key` (`:171`); `DPLANT.plant_region` holds one region per plant (`:91`).

**A4 KPIs.** `WMAPE = Σ|PL−AC| / ΣPL` per month, SUMX over `DSALES_Product` (ops: MK), blank when AC = 0; lag = date − N×30; LN only a slicer [pbi].

**A5 Planners' accuracy** (indicative, baseline universe; recomputed on U in Phase 10). PAL / keep = cell WMAPE at LN × PM (ops: plant × MK); control = ACMELIFT-exact SUMX; net bias = Σ(PL−AC)/ΣPL; lag 6 on scope = mapped [baseline; csv]. Requested production here is scored against sales `Production_units_total` I, the reconciliation series (DF-08).

**A6 Access today [md].** The Power BI Sales model has row-level security: role `New role` filters `vw_gt_access_snops_product` (→ `Product Division`) and `vw_gt_access_snops_region` (→ sales `Region`) by `[email_address] = USERPRINCIPALNAME()`, security group `SG-APP-IBP`; the Ops model has none. dEPM writes are gated per dimension by OLAP roles (`DSALES_DIVISION_WRITE`, `DSALES_REGION_WRITE`, `DSALES_PRODUCT_WRITE`, `DPLANT`, `DMANUFACT_KEY`); the Accountability Matrix lists 166 `Active Infor Users`. Row counts of the RLS views were not exported.

**A7 Configuration and components today.** dEPM plans finished units per PM and MK only; the plan exports carry no BOM, routing, lead-time or component field [xlsx]. The vault classes `-SK-`, `-EK-`, `-ST-`, `-SL-` keys as SAP configurable materials and `-P000x` keys as fixed configurations [vault]; on the MK dimension 150 of 1,365 IDs carry a configurable suffix and 30 a `-P0xx` suffix [measured r98] (a naming rule, not a verified configurable flag). Configuration (e.g. colour) is known only on a real SAP order [gate2r2 OQ-C], and no M1 input carries it (U1). The vault names "long-lead components" as the reason to look at the 4-6-month horizon [vault], and the adopted design brief asks for typed dated item links with a quota "measured from order-book mix over a stated window with manual override distinguishable" [vault]. No source read names a component, an option group, a rate or a phantom assembly: the Planning BOM content is user knowledge still to be supplied (U9). The chain PM → MK → plant → semi-finished is spread today over text attributes on dimension rows [md], the bridge, the BA-kit Excel file [inputs §1] and the central re-allocation by hand [vault]; the master-data specification written for 9.8.2 models it as separate dimensions and links — PM D-04, MK D-05, component D-13, bridge L-02, MK → plant L-03, Region Model Assignment L-06, PM → plant L-07, Planning BOM L-11…L-14 [md-spec] — with no view that reads them as one path.

### Located defects
#### System (the rigid mechanics M1 replaces)
| ID | Defect (observable) | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| DS1 | A demand change reaches plant load only after the day-10 handover, confirmation after day 16, sales-side inventory only after jobs 3300/3100 and the §3.3 batch: one demand → supply → inventory round per month | vault; ana050 §3.3; inputs §2, §4 |
| DS2 | No what-if object: instances come from copy jobs (2000/3000/4000/4100), no branch is recorded; Gartner 2024: technology 1, overall 2 vs peer overall 3- | vault; corpus |
| DS3 | Manual steps outside the system every cycle: YCRANE back-allocation correction, BA-kit and installation allocation files, dependent demand, re-allocation to supplying plants | vault; gate2 OQ-17; inputs §1 |
| DS4 | The order book enters only the P instance (Sales mask, as of m−1 end); C at month end reflects the month-start order book | gate2 OQ-8; inputs §3 |
| DS5 | The instance chain produces near-copies: R ≈ P (FVA ≤ 0.03 pp), A = C (15 / 500,698 keys) | baseline 2, 3.5 |
| DS6 | Inventory, FMS and the effect of a production change appear only after a "Refresh FMS & inventory" button or a job run; the production term differs between masks (requested in Sales, confirmed in Ops) | inputs §2; fms |
| DS7 | Inside the frozen zone "no production request can change": when a firm order's date moves (a late order), Requested production stays in its old month, so inventory, load and gap show the order where it no longer is, until the zone has rolled past it; how often this happens is not measured | vault; inputs §1; gate2r12 |
| DS8 | **No operations order book exists** — a pain point named by the user [gate2r18]: the ops planner sees the order book only read-only, at sales grain, from the Sales cycle; the assembly plants' own sales orders (the Operations Orderbook [gate2r13]) are kept nowhere in S&OP; the ops cube's `OrderBook_units` at plant × MK (461,127 rows) is a copy of the sales order book distributed to plants *(inference until the U10 export)*, so a plant's late or moved order cannot be seen at plant × MK | inputs §1; ops-extract; ops tmdl; gate2r12; gate2r13; gate2r18 |
| DS9 | No component requirement for configurable products: S&OP plans finished units only, so the units of each component option (e.g. which pump) a plan implies are computed nowhere; no BOM, routing or component field exists in the plan exports; 150 of 1,365 MK IDs carry a configurable-material suffix; long-lead components are named as a reason for the 4-6-month horizon | xlsx; vault; measured r98; gate2r14 |
| DS10 | The chain PM → MK (weights) → plant → semi-finished → components cannot be read as one path: links are text attributes on dimension rows, the semi-finished step lives in an Excel file, the plant split in a manual re-allocation; the user asked for one element table with everything, then for one item master [gate2r16] | md; inputs §1; vault; gate2r14; gate2r16 |
| DS11 | The sales planner cannot see where a planning model is produced: the sales mask filters are Year, Month, Cycle, Region, Product Line, with no plant or cluster; the product carries one text `Plant` attribute (product → plants {1: 684, 2: 1}) while keys run at 2 plants ({1: 668, 2: 10}; 23 multi-plant pairs); and since 9.7 the frozen zone lives per MK × plant while the sales mask shows a planning-model cell, so a cell frozen at one plant and open at the other had no defined display | inputs §1; xlsx; vault; critic r7; gate2r16 |
| DS12 | A new model enters the plan through separate admin steps that nobody reviews together: element creation in dEPM (`DSALES_PRODUCT_WRITE`), the "Link Manufacturing Key to Plant" report, Region Model Assignment and SOP / EOP masks, mapping configuration; the user wants new models created and entered into the plan inside the Portfolio Review | md; inputs §1; ana050 §3.3; gate2r18 |
| DS13 | Which plant supplies which region is held nowhere as a rule: it is encoded in 36 plant-suffixed and 21 `-BA` planning-model IDs (a plant and a region choice made by naming a separate model), in the single-valued `Targeted Plant` attribute (≠ the supplying plant on 38 of 490 pairs) and in the monthly manual re-allocation; a region that may take a model from several plants with one preferred (LATAM, APAC [gate2r17]) cannot be stated | vault; xlsx; md; gate2r17 |

| DM8 | AC5 and GRANA-01 in `.planning/REQUIREMENTS.md` (and ROADMAP Phase 8 criterion 2) still say a non-eligible plant "viene rifiutato" with a typed error, against CP-07 and the master-data link L-03 ("accepted and flagged … never blocks"); AC6 asks for capacity in both plants in a phase that has no capacity nodes yet | req; md-spec; gate2r14 |
| DM9 | The master-data specification (written for 9.8.2) models planning models, MKs and components as three dimensions (D-04, D-05, D-13) and the bridge (L-02), MK → plant (L-03), Region Model Assignment (L-06), PM → plant (L-07) and the Planning BOM (L-11…L-14) as separate links, with MK and PM parameters P-01…P-05: superseded by the item master of DF-12, so Phase 5's schema would follow a model the user has replaced | md-spec; gate2r16 |

## Requirements (each traced to a defect and its evidence)

Tags: **[UM]** user-mandated scope · **[PREF]** preference · **[AD]** threshold or rule set by the architect, provisional · **[AD orchestrator, gate2r14]** / **[AD orchestrator, gate2r16]** rule decided by the orchestrator on the user's behalf · **[gate2r17]** / **[gate2r18 <question ID>]** user decision of 2026-10-09 / 2026-10-12 · **U#** depends on an action · **OQ-n** depends on an open question with an [AD] default. Data role per T3: *in* = planner input, *calc* = calculated, *tv* = training / verification only, *md* = master data.

### CP — Concurrent engine (critical path)
| ID | Requirement | Closes | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| CP-01 | One concurrent DAG from the planner inputs (Sales / Invoices, Requested production, Confirmed production, the opening inventory and the Operations Orderbook) through the inventory row and FMS (CP-03), requested load at MK × plant × region × month, slots and hours vs capacity (NW-06), **gap requested-confirmed at MK × plant × month**, value gap in local currency and EUR (CP-09), and the frozen-zone default of Requested (CP-08); no job or refresh button. Every node runs over the **plan horizon** = the open months of the current FY plus the whole next FY (24 months in January, 13 in December; FY = calendar year [gate2r12]) [gate2r11], bucketed on the DF-13 calendar; the closed months of the current FY carry actuals and are read-only | DS1, DS6, DC2, DD6, DK4 | vault; code; inputs §1, §2; gate2r11; gate2r12 |
| CP-02 | **Inventory row** at LE × region × planning model × month: end(m) = end(m−1) − Sales / Invoices(m) + production(m), where Sales / Invoices are **external** units only, so stock leaves the plan perimeter only on a third-party invoice [vault]; production = Requested or Confirmed by a switch per view and per scenario (default: requested for sales roles, confirmed for ops roles, as in the masks), which resolves the Sales-vs-Ops mask contradiction; until Phase 6 builds the confirmed side, confirmed views fall back to requested and are labelled so. **Opening.** Month 1 starts from the **opening used in the calculation = Total + FGNI** [gate2r12], landed through the input layer (DF-10: the opening-inventory mask, ERP extract or Snowflake) at LE × region × planning model [gate2r5; gate2r8]: **Financial** and **IC** (any sign; may be negative) typed by the sales side; **Total = Financial + IC**, calculated, and the figure an opening import carries [gate2r12]; **FGNI** = finished units held in an assembly plant at m−1 end, produced and not yet invoiced to the sales entity that will sell them, entered by the ops planner of the plant [gate2r13], keyed plant × destination LE × region × PM and summed over plants into the opening cell [gate2r13]. **Sign rule [AD]:** Financial and FGNI are ≥ 0 when typed by a planner or landed through channels 2-3, a negative value is rejected and counted; from the pilot-operator fixture channel a negative Financial is accepted (stored DWH inventory carries reporting corrections, E5), flagged "negative fixture" and counted. **No double count [gate2r13]:** because the UM's IC also covers "plant stock to be invoiced to you" [inputs §2], from M1 on IC excludes units still held in an assembly plant; those arrive only as FGNI, and S-06 says so in an info pop-up (UX-01). An FGNI arrival keyed only plant × MK (channels 2-3, or a plant that does not know the destination) is mapped MK → PM through the bridge (NW-01; N:1 by request share as NW-02) and spread to LE × region on the coarse-opening basis below, provenance "mapped" (Phase 6) [gate2r13; mapping rule AD]; FGNI not reported by a plant for m−1 counts as 0, is flagged per plant and goes on the PR-05 agenda [AD]. A missing Total is a missing opening: counted, flagged, the row shows "opening missing". **Coarser opening — fallback only [AD]:** the mask channel lands at PM; a channel 2-3 arrival coarser than LE × region × PM (their grains are unknown, U5 / U6; the UM mask's Division × product line is the documented example) is spread to the LE × region × PM cells inside it in proportion to (1) the **current cycle's plan Sales / Invoices at LE × region × PM summed over months m..m+2** (common plan of the cycle, opened from the previous published R, PR-01; negative cells weigh 0); else (2) the current cycle's Requested production at the same grain and months; both zero → rejected with SC-02's typed error and counted as missing. Both bases are operational-store inputs at the opening's target grain; the DWH is never a basis. Spread and mapped values carry their provenance, are fixed at landing (a later plan edit does not re-spread) and can be overwritten cell by cell. Every value is logged (user, time, value, previous value, channel, source). FMS is computed from the selected row by CP-03, never entered. Compare and KPIs carry the switch: two scenarios compare on the same switch; every inventory / FMS figure is labelled requested or confirmed. **Merged planning models [gate2r17]:** openings and actuals landed on a merged dEPM alias (plant-suffixed or `-BR`, T12) sum onto the base planning model's cell; they carry no plant. Verification only: the DWH holds one inventory number per cell (A2), so fixtures land it as Financial with IC = 0 and FGNI = 0 [AD]; with that opening, the formula reproduces 17,407 / 17,408 cells [critic r2] | DS6, DC2, DD9, N1, N4 | inputs §1, §2; pbi; vault; gate2 OQ-9; gate2r4; gate2r5; gate2r6; gate2r8; gate2r12; gate2r13; critic r3, r4, r7 |
| CP-03 | Requested production is entered, as today, and on the frozen shares of a cell defaults to the Operations Orderbook (CP-08); **no derived mode from an inventory target**: no inventory target exists. **FMS node, dEPM logic kept [gate2r8]:** FMS(m) at LE × region × PM × month = number of whole months from m+1 until the cumulative Sales / Invoices of the same scenario or plan reaches end(m), plus the linear fraction `(end(m) − cumulative before) / Sales(crossing month)`; blank sales = 0; end(m) ≤ 0 → 0 [fms]; end(m) is the CP-02 row, so the opening used is Total + FGNI. Requested FMS reads the requested row (reproduces `FMSRate_calc`); the confirmed FMS reads the confirmed row from Phase 6 (reproduces `FMSRate_conf_calc`). **Roll-ups** — Division "-" (model × region over all LEs), product line (`Crane (E)` …), any compare or KPI group, and any read scope (UX-03) — apply the same formula to summed inventory and summed sales; FMS is never summed or averaged across cells. An edit to Sales / Invoices in month k recomputes FMS for all months before k whose window reaches k (DAG edges cover the forward window). **Horizon edge and defaults [AD]:** the forward window stops at the end of the next FY (CP-01), so months near that edge have fewer forward months; a cell not covered before the edge gets the months to the edge, flagged "not covered" — more cells are flagged in December than in January. dEPM's stored countdown to a far fixed horizon is not reproduced; Phase 5 reports the gap. No rounding; the sales series is the scenario's own. Power BI's FMS DAX is a reporting variant and is not followed. Stored `FMSRate_*` values are used only to verify; stored FMS of merged aliases (T12) is not additive, so merged planning models are listed outside the reproduction | N3, E23, DS6, DD1 | fms; gate2r3 OQ-B'; gate2r8; gate2r11; gate2r12; inputs §2 |
| CP-04 | Incremental = full recalculation on the real-size perimeter (AC10), early cutoff (AC11), group recomputation of min/max/ratio (AC12) | regression | code |
| CP-05 | Set-based build over the real-size perimeter: AC29's < 25 queries kept; AC29's < 5 s on the engine profile; on real-size data [AD] build ≤ 60 s, edit-to-consequence ≤ 2 s, measured in wave 1 (explicit amendment of AC29's time) | DC4 | code; corpus |
| CP-06 | Engine keys carry region from the input, never from the division; a row with region "-" keeps "-", no region invented (subsumes GRANA-01's grain; its "blocking precondition of AC5" clause is superseded with AC5, CP-07). **Region = the L-2 node of the sales region hierarchy** [gate2r18 MD-Q-2]: an input or fixture row on a member below L-2 lands on its L-2 ancestor, totals preserved, counted [AD]; the same level keys ITEM × REGION, the sourcing rule (NW-08) and the region read scope (UX-03). **No region is fixed on an item [gate2r16]:** the one-value `item.region` column and `item.is_active` are dropped; which regions a planning model is planned in, and when, lives on its ITEM × REGION rows (DF-12); a sales row on a planning model × region without an active row lands, is flagged "outside Region Model Assignment" and counted, never rejected [AD] | DC3, DC13, P4, B10 | code; xlsx; corpus; schema; md; gate2r16; gate2r18 |
| CP-07 | Plant by evidence and by rules the planners keep. **Precedence, highest first** (made explicit in 9.8.1 [critic r9], restated in 9.9.1 [critic r10; AD orchestrator, gate2r16], re-ordered in 9.9.2 [gate2r17]): (1) **planner override**, traced (AC8), including an **alias pin** (lifecycle in T12: an ordinary CP-08 share once landed, fixture cycles only) — a row landed on the dEPM ID of a merged planning model (plant-suffixed or `-BR`, T12) is pinned to the plant that alias names, provenance "alias pin" [AD]; (2) for an MK whose Planning BOM has a **routing group**, that group — ordered units by their real configuration, the remainder by the group's rates (NW-07) — overriding tiers 3-4 [AD orchestrator, gate2r14]; a routing option outside the region's allowed plants is accepted and flagged "routing outside sourcing" [AD]; (3) the **regional sourcing rule** of the MK × the cell's region in force (NW-08) [gate2r17]: its default shares where given, else 100 % on its preferred plant; (4) the **master split** (*md*, DF-12) [AD orchestrator, gate2r16] for an MK × region without a rule: an MK with **more than one** active ITEM × PLANT row in the month splits by the rows' **default plant shares**, and the product `Plant` attribute is not read; an MK with **exactly one** row takes that row, and only there is the product `Plant` attribute read — a product `Plant` that differs from the single row is accepted and flagged as below. **Seed of the default shares**, for folded and non-folded multi-plant items alike: the share of Confirmed units per plant over the last 12 closed months — for a folded item the plant of each history row is its ID's suffix plant, the fact plant only where the ID has no suffix; no Confirmed history → 100 % on the plant of the product `Plant` attribute if that plant is one of the item's rows, else tier 5. The shares are dated master data the global supply planner and the data manager can change [gate2r18 MD-Q-10]. Tier 4 is the default for MKs × regions without a rule and for a routing MK's units not covered by a routing rate in force; (5) `UNASSIGNED` with volume, on the PR-05 agenda. The **demand-side plant qualifier** of 9.9-9.9.1 is retired: the plant suffixes and `-BA` finishing plants it carried are now alias pins on history and sourcing rules for the plan [gate2r17]. Inside a frozen MK × plant pair the CP-08 frozen share governs that pair's units; the rule above splits the open part [AD]. No `ANY_SCOPE`, no RULE tier. **AC5 realigned [gate2r14]:** one MK may be produced at several plants — its ITEM × PLANT rows (DF-12; formerly the MK → plant list L-03 [md-spec]) —; a plant outside those rows — by planner override, an alias pin, a landed Confirmed or Operations Orderbook row, a routing option, a sourcing rule or a single-plant MK's product `Plant` — is **accepted and flagged** "new key × plant pair", counted and listed (master-data check DQ-26 [md-spec]), **never rejected**; an override to a plant not allowed by the region's sourcing rule is accepted and flagged "outside sourcing rule" [AD]. The old AC5 wording ("un plant non eleggibile viene rifiutato", ROADMAP Phase 8 criterion 2: "rifiutata con un errore tipizzato") and GRANA-01 ("precondizione bloccante di AC5") in `.planning/REQUIREMENTS.md` are **superseded**: AC5 reads as stated here, GRANA-01's grain survives as CP-06; the stage-6 GSD ingest rewrites both texts [req] | DM2, DM8, DC3, DC14, DD11, DS13, P1, P7, P9, P11, P12 | corpus; gate2 OQ-11; gate2r14; gate2r16; gate2r17; gate2r18; md-spec; req; critic r9; critic r10 |
| CP-08 | **Frozen zone: visible, defaulted from the Operations Orderbook on the frozen MK × plant shares only, exceptions by reason** [gate2r12; critic r7, rule decided by the orchestrator on the user's behalf]. **Zone per MK × plant pair** — an ITEM × PLANT row of the MK item (DF-12) [gate2r16]: the months that start before today + the pair's zone length, counted in the Working Days entered by Ops for that plant × the MK's line key; a month partly inside is frozen [AD]; zone length = master data on the ITEM × PLANT row, dated, **given by Operations per MK × plant** [gate2r18 MD-Q-7]; a length Operations gives for an MK without a plant is written to every plant row of the item [AD]; empty until the list arrives (U4) [UM]. Zone ends are computed on the DF-13 calendar. The zone stays visible in every mask (marked, not hidden, not locked), and is shown to sales per plant as UX-05 states [gate2r16]. **Frozen share of a planning-model cell:** a Requested cell at LE × region × PM × month is split into MK × plant shares on a **non-circular basis** [critic r8; AD]: (1) the cell's **pre-default request** — the split of the value the planner entered (or the common plan carried) before any order-book default touched the cell, kept per share — where it exists; else (2) the **CP-07 split (tiers 1-4) × the bridge link weights** (alias pins, routing rates, the cell region's sourcing rule, ITEM × PLANT default shares; YCRANE N:1 by request %). A sourcing-rule or default-share change dated inside a pair's zone does not move that pair's frozen share; it applies to open shares and to months after the zone [AD]. The requested load inside the zone is itself the default and is never used as the split basis. An N:1 YCRANE key, a multi-plant key (P1) or an item folded from plant-suffixed keys (B5: `CIT-11-01-000` with ITEM × PLANT rows Oakridge and Northfield, formerly two keys) give several pairs; for P11 the pair is the supplying plant, not the targeted one [AD]. For each pair whose **own** zone covers the month, that share is set automatically to the **Operations Orderbook** units of that MK × plant — the assembly plant's own sales orders [gate2r13], which have no history in any source and land by ops-planner entry or, later, an ERP extract (DF-10; in the pilot a labelled proxy) [gate2r18] —, placed in the month of each order's **latest updated date** and allocated to this cell — rows carrying LE × region × PM set it directly, others in proportion to the cells' shares of that pair on the same non-circular basis, largest remainder, `-BA` with its 2-month offset [AD]. Shares of pairs outside their zone keep the planner's request; the cell = frozen shares + open shares. When an order's date moves (a late order, V9) its units move at the next landing, and inventory, FMS, load and gap follow. The default carries provenance "order book" and is recomputed on every Operations Orderbook landing (PR-02). **Planner edits:** an edit of the cell total is spread over its open shares in proportion to them [AD]; it is an **exception** only if it changes a frozen share — the cell has no open share (or all open shares are 0), or the planner edits a frozen share in the MK × plant breakdown. An exception needs a reason (UX dialog S-17) or is rejected; it is logged (user, time, value, default value, reason), carries provenance "manual exception", survives later order-book landings until removed [AD], and, once confirmed into the common plan, is flagged on the cockpit agenda (PR-05) and raises a PR-06 alert to ops. Who may except: the Requested owner within write scope (sales planner) [AD]. **UNASSIGNED plant (CP-07):** that share has no zone and no frozen default; the cell goes on the PR-05 agenda when the month lies within the zone length of its MK at any plant [AD]. If no Operations Orderbook has landed for a pair, its last default stays, labelled "order book missing"; a pair that never had one keeps the planner's request, labelled the same [AD]. Outside every zone Requested is entered as today | V8, V9, V10, DS7, DD10, DD11, P1, B5, P11, DS13, P12 | vault; inputs §1; gate2r2 OQ-22; gate2r12; gate2r13; gate2r16; gate2r17; gate2r18; critic r7, r8, r10 |
| CP-09 | **Value gap in local currency and EUR** [gate2r18 UX-Q-7]: value gap(LE × region × PM × month) = (Requested − Confirmed back-allocated by NW-02) × price, in the **local currency of the LE** (the company code's `Currency` attribute [md]), converted to **EUR** with the FX rate of that currency × month (DF-14). **Price basis [gate2r19 OQ-30]:** a **price table** kept by the data manager (DF-14 mask): average price per **planning model or Product Class** (e.g. KBC 10-20 tm), in the LE's local currency, dated by month of validity; a planning-model price wins over its Product Class price [AD]; no dEPM price cube is needed. Until prices are entered the value gap is blank and counted. Values in different currencies are never summed: roll-ups across LEs, the ops view at MK × plant (sum of its back-allocated cells) and compare totals are in EUR; a cell without price or FX rate has no value, is flagged and counted on the PR-05 agenda [AD]. Computed from Phase 6, when back-allocation exists; before that only the units gap is shown [AD]. Replaces the seed `value_gap` node | DC15, N5 | gate2r18; gate2r19; md; code |

### SC — User scenarios (critical path)
| ID | Requirement | Closes | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| SC-01 | A planner creates a named scenario on the common plan or on another scenario and edits at input grain (sales: LE × region × planning model × month; ops: the DF-10 ops grains — Confirmed at plant × MK × month, capacity at plant × MK × month, Working Days at plant × line key × month), within the role's write scope (UX-03) and the open months of the plan horizon (CP-01). R-001 Step B item 1 moves into M1 by an explicit ADR-001 amendment | DC5, DS2 | arch; gate2 OQ-10, OQ-13; inputs §1 |
| SC-02 | An aggregate edit spreads to input grain in proportion to the scenario's current values; a zero aggregate is rejected with a typed error naming the grain to use. A coarser opening inventory is spread on the CP-02 basis instead; a Requested cell with frozen shares spreads only over its open shares (CP-08); an **Order Intake forecast** is entered at the level the data manager sets — product line, product family or planning model [gate2r18 UX-Q-20]; default product line, "product family" = Product Class [gate2r19 OQ-31], one setting per product line, a change applying from the next cycle [AD]; values are always stored at LE × region × PM; an entry at an aggregate whose cells are all zero (the dEPM product-category level, S9) spreads on the same cells' plan Sales / Invoices of the same months, and is rejected only if those are zero too [AD] | S3, S9 | ana050 §3.4; arch; gate2r5; gate2r13; gate2r18; critic r7 |
| SC-03 | A cell without base row can be created in a scenario (Phase 5) and confirmed into the common plan (Phase 8) | DC5, S4 | arch |
| SC-04 | Compare scenario vs common plan vs scenarios, every measure, by LN, region, PM, MK, plant; non-additive recomputed on the group, else `not_comparable` with reason (AC28) | DC6, N2 | code |
| SC-05 | Traceability and **three visibility levels** [gate2r9]: **personal** (Draft, visible only to its author), **Shared for review** (read-only, visible to reviewers and, within their scope, to view-only roles), **Confirmed** (committed into the common plan, visible to sales and ops at once), or Discarded. One parent pointer per node; lifecycle and cycle orthogonal; per-row provenance after confirm; every input, edit, share, confirm and publish has authenticated user, time, reason, source; confirm records the approver (R-001 Step A, AC31). Plan figures typed in a mask land in the author's working scenario; facts (actuals, openings, sales order book, Operations Orderbook) land in the common plan directly [AD]. Principle private / share / commit from the vendors [vendors D; vendors C]; triggers and mechanism ours [gate2r4] | DC7, S7, DS1 | arch; project; proposal; vendors D; vendors C; gate2r9 |
| SC-06 | Common plan moved (m−1 actuals, order-book update, a change confirmed by another scenario or the other function, new cycle): conflict detected before confirm (AC16); rebase replays the edit log, vanished cells reported | S6 | arch; gate G5; gate2r9 |
| SC-07 | **Two acts, kept apart [gate2r9].** **Confirm a change:** from Shared for review by an approver, or by the data owner for changes on its own role's data [AD]; commits the scenario's edits into the cycle's **common plan** after the SC-06 conflict check; visible to both roles at once; raises PR-06 alerts; logged with approver. **Monthly publish:** by the approver at the monthly review (PR-01), the common plan is frozen into the official versions `CYCLE MM YYYY R` (demand layer) and `CYCLE MM YYYY C` (supply layer) [gate2r2 OQ-A; gate2r7], names derived from the cycle, never typed. Identity per DF-03 = (cycle, instance, origin `published`, sequence); the name is an alias, so each coexists with the read-only reference of the same name loaded from history (origin `loaded`), as V6's "one identity, two aliases". **One current published version per cycle × instance [AD]:** a re-publish supersedes it; the previous one stays read-only and kept, both logged, the handoff file re-issued with the sequence. Publish writes the dEPM handoff file (AC24) in the extract fact layout under the official names, with every item and item × plant key written under its dEPM ID alias (DF-12, T12) and the demand rows of a merged planning model written under its dEPM IDs by the alias split (T12) [gate2r17]; if dEPM needs P and A they are written as copies of R and C [AD] (history: R ≈ P, A = C, V1, V2); a dry-run file can be written at any time, marked so. Scenarios and the unpublished common plan never reach Power BI. No dEPM or DWH `version_name` rewritten in M1; in the tool, uniqueness moves from the name to the identity (identity refactor, Plan). In-place publish into dEPM = M2 target | S7, S8, DS2, DS5, DC9 | pbi; corpus; gate2r2 OQ-A, OQ-H; gate2r7; gate2r9; gate2r16; critic r5; proposal |
| SC-08 | Re-entrant transactions (AC30) | DC8 | project |

### DF — Data foundation
| ID | Requirement | Closes | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| DF-01 | Two stores, one direction: an **operational store** fed only by the input layer (DF-10, three channels) and master data; a **training / verification store** loaded read-only from the DWH extracts (Parquet → local gitignored DuckDB). The engine never reads the training store at runtime; it reaches the operational store only as calibrated profiles and seeded master data (CP-07 default shares, NW-05, QC-04) or as fixtures pushed through the input layer. The boundary is by role and channel, not technology: "DWH" = the Azure SQL `dmsnop` views and the Power BI models on them; Snowflake is an operational channel (DF-10 channel 3) | DC9; REQ-F4 | state; project; gate2r5; gate2r6 |
| DF-02 | LN first-class (many codes per LN — 132 LNs over 142 codes, 6 LNs with several codes [md]; LE = Sales Division → Legal Name); region an independent axis, keyed at L-2 (CP-06); LEs active for planning = those with plan facts [gate2r18 MD-Q-11] | DC1, P4, E8 | pbi; xlsx; inputs §1; md |
| DF-03 | Version identity (cycle, side, instance \| reference type \| scenario \| common plan; origin `loaded` \| `published` with sequence) with Infor and DWH aliases; names are aliases, never keys: the schema is unique on identity, not on `version_name`, and no code path looks a version up by name (identity refactor, Plan) | DC9, V6, S8 | xlsx; pbi; gate2r7; gate2r9; critic r5 |
| DF-04 | **Element-ID identity on the item master (DF-12)**: an item is identified by its origin — the dEPM dimension it comes from (`DSALES_Product`, `DMANUFACT_Key`), "folded" for an MK item merged from plant-suffixed keys, "merged" for a planning-model item merged from plant-suffixed or `-BR` planning models whose base ID does not exist (T12), or "tool" for components and phantoms — and its element ID, so a planning model and an MK with the same ID string (B1: 374 1:1 identical) stay two items [AD]; caption a label; dated aliases for items, plants and keys (D-003, D-004), and, for an item folded or merged from suffixed IDs, each dEPM ID as a dated alias **of its ITEM × PLANT row** (MK) or **of (planning model, plant)** (merged planning model) [gate2r16; gate2r17]; at any date an alias resolves to exactly one item, ITEM × PLANT row or (planning model, plant); the **28 MK IDs with a `-BA` pattern [md] are seeded as items of classification semi-finished** — the basic assemblies of the two-step, never created by hand [AD orchestrator, gate2r16; semi-finished key + finishing plant confirmed, gate2r18 MD-Q-20] — *(inference: the 28 are selected by regex; Phase 5 checks the 3 Basic Assemblies of B11 against them and lists the 28 for the data manager to confirm before Phase 6 links them [critic r11])*; MK → line key = `Product Line` + " (E)", L-2 fallback (100 % of capacity history [measured r7b]); product lines = 14 planning lines + 5 other values [gate2r18 MD-Q-17]. **Seed:** master data seeded once from the DWH dimension exports, the DWH the primary source [gate2r18 MD-Q-5]; ITEM × REGION (Region Model Assignment, SOP / EOP) from the dEPM "parameters" cube once exported (U11) [gate2r18 MD-Q-4], else from the planning model × region pairs with plan facts in the last cycle, SOP / EOP empty [AD]; ITEM × PLANT rows (the MK → plant links) seeded once from the distinct MK × plant pairs of Confirmed history [gate2r18 MD-Q-10]; sourcing rules per NW-08; dealer flag from the company view [gate2r18 MD-Q-14]; display order kept, German labels not carried [gate2r18 MD-Q-13]; subcontractor = a plant under `External` [gate2r18 MD-Q-9]; plant → region seeded from the plant dimension's `Region` attribute with its 5 nulls flagged [md]. **Maintenance:** by the S&OP data manager (ITEM, ITEM × REGION, Region Model Assignment and SOP / EOP, the calendar of DF-13, FX of DF-14, users, roles, scopes, plant → region), with the **global supply planner** on ITEM × PLANT rows, default plant shares and sourcing rules [gate2r18 MD-Q-10; sourcing AD] and **Operations** on hour capacity, Average Assembly Hours, week-slot parameters [gate2r18 MD-Q-8] and Planning BOM rates [gate2r18 UX-Q-24], as specified by DF-11 | DD5, DD7, DD10, DD11 | xlsx; | DF-09 | Production alert on received actuals, whatever the channel, for each closed month. **Ops side:** Produced Units at plant × region × MK (DF-08). **Sales side — same identity as CP-02 [critic r7, decided by the orchestrator on the user's behalf]:** production implied by the sales actuals = Δ inventory + **External** invoiced units. Own Region and Group invoices are intra-group [AD]: they move stock between LEs and never leave the group, so they are not depletion; they cancel when every LE of the bridge group is summed (example: an LE invoices 10 units to a group entity that keeps them in stock — the sender's stock falls by 10, the receiver's rises by 10, the group nets to 0; with Total as depletion the receiver would show 10 units of false implied production). The **Invoiced Total** is typed by the planner (it may differ from External + Own Region + Group, the difference flagged; DF-10, [gate2r13]); it is shown and imported, never used for depletion. It is not the inventory Total, which is Financial + IC and calculated (CP-02). **Comparison through the PM ↔ MK bridge (NW-01), not a product-line roll-up [AD]:** both sides are summed to the **bridge group** — the smallest set of PMs and MKs closed under bridge links — × month, with every LE summed on the sales side (this is where intra-group flows net out) and every plant on the ops side; **region is summed by default** because an intra-group invoice can cross regions [AD]; the region breakdown is shown, and the alert moves to bridge group × region × month only if Phase 6 measures cross-region intra-group volume as negligible and reading (i) resolves. A 1:1 link (B1-B3) is a group of one PM and one MK; N:1 (B4 YCRANE) collapses into one group; B5, now one MK item with two ITEM × PLANT rows (DF-12), is a group of one PM and one MK whose plants are summed; plant-suffixed, `-BA`, `-BR` keys (B6-B8) follow their link class, `-BA` with its 2-month offset; one-side-only IDs (B9) and semi-finished keys without PM (B11, and the `-BA` semi-finished items) have nothing to compare against, so they are counted and listed, not alerted. A difference ≥ 1 unit [AD] raises an alert the planner approves, with the PM, MK and region breakdown of both sides shown; log keeps user, time, both values, delta, note; open alerts go to the PR-05 agenda. Two readings are inferences, measured on history once the bridge exists (Phase 6) [AD]: (i) Ops actuals region = plant's region or destination region; (ii) Δ inventory = Δ of the opening used in the calculation, **Financial + IC + FGNI** (default, because with FGNI the produced-not-invoiced units are no longer lost between entities [gate2r12]), with Financial + IC and Financial only printed alongside; the default switches if history contradicts. Two residuals do not net inside one group and are measured and printed in Phase 6 [critic r8]: units invoiced intra-group at month end but not yet in the receiver's stock *(inference)*, and **cross-group intra-group volume** — an intra-group invoice whose sender and receiver rows fall on different bridge groups. The DWH Δ 13,109 (sales `Production_units_total` I vs ops `C_Total` I) is the verification evidence that the alert is needed; its expected volume per bridge group is measured on history in Phase 6 | DD9, E24 | gate2r3 OQ-G'; gate2r5; gate2r8; gate2r11; gate2r12; gate2r13; critic r7, r8, r9; inputs §1, §2; critic r3; xlsx; vault |

The contract is identical per channel: target measure and grain, element-ID key resolution (dEPM IDs through the DF-04 aliases to an item or an ITEM × PLANT row), validation (grain, type, sign per CP-02, lock, frozen zone, horizon, the author's write scope) with rejects counted per channel, log (user, time, value, previous value), provenance (channel, source file or object, extraction time); no value enters the operational store any other way. Plan figures land in the author's working scenario, facts in the common plan (SC-05). Lock and frozen-zone validations are contract hooks: stubbed (always open; empty zone list) until PR-03 (Phase 9) and CP-08 (Phase 7, U4) fill them. **Masks show the two-FY horizon:** the closed months of the current FY as read-only actuals next to the open months of the current FY and the whole next FY as plan (24 months in January, 13 in December) [gate2r11]. Measures and grains: **Sales planning** (Sales / Invoices = external units only [vault], **Order Intake forecast** [gate2r13] — a plan figure over the same horizon, entered like Sales / Invoices, of external orders [gate2r15] (the stored measure is labelled "Order Intake [external]" on every row [measured r981]), no engine consequence beyond display, compare and publish, written to the handoff on the "(E)" product member dEPM stores it on [AD; U2], an aggregate entry spread per SC-02 —, Order Book (with order-line configuration where the U1 feed carries it: the configuration source of NW-07), Requested Production; **LE × region (L-2) × planning model × month**, as CP-02, CP-06 and SC-01; the Order Intake forecast entered at the level set per SC-02 [gate2r18 UX-Q-20]; with filters Year, Month, Cycle, Region, Product Line and, from 9.9, **Plant and Cluster** with sorting by them (UX-05) [gate2r16]; frozen shares visible, defaulted per CP-08, with the MK × plant breakdown of each cell and the three-state display of UX-05); **Sales actuals** m−1 at **LE × region (L-2) × planning model** [gate2r8; gate2r18 MD-Q-2], not the UM mask's Division × product line: **Invoiced and Order Intake by External / Own Region / Group / Total, all four typed** [gate2r11; gate2r13] — Total is not calculated; the mask shows Total − (External + Own Region + Group) and flags a non-zero difference without rejecting it [AD]; Total for display and import only, depletion uses External (CP-02, DF-09) —, Orderbook with Own Region + Group typed, External from the planning mask, Total and the Check column calculated [inputs §1; gate2r18 UX-Q-14]; **Opening inventory, its own mask (UX S-06)** [gate2r12] at LE × region × PM: Financial and IC typed (sales side), Total = Financial + IC calculated, FGNI (ops side, per plant and destination), opening used in the calculation = Total + FGNI, all per CP-02 (a coarser arrival is spread, CP-02); **Ops planning, one key set [AD; critic r7]:** Confirmed Production at **plant × MK × month**, no region (B10; T4's `(MK, plant, month)`) — the key of an ITEM × PLANT row (DF-12); Cluster average max capacity per day at plant × MK × month (leaf keys, Basic Assembly cluster keys included); Working Days at plant × line key × month; the UM's Region × product line × plant (cluster rows) are filters and row groupings of the mask, not keys; **Operations Orderbook** [gate2r12] = **the sales orders of the assembly plants** [gate2r13] — the open order lines each plant holds for units it will build —, seen and entered by the ops planner, a fact landing in the common plan, at **plant × MK × month of the order's latest updated date**, with the ordering LE × region (the sold-to entity of the order line *(inference)*) and the PM per row where the source has them, else the PM from MK through the bridge (NW-01, N:1 by request share as NW-02) [AD]; **no history of it exists in any source** — the ops cube `OrderBook_units` is a copy of the sales order book [gate2r18] —, so live values come only from ops-planner entry (channel 1) and, later, an ERP extract of the plants' sales-order lines (channel 2, U5) or Snowflake (U6); **pilot fixtures [AD]:** a proxy landed from the ops cube `OrderBook_units` (its plant × MK rows), labelled "proxy: sales order book", so the frozen-zone default runs at real size while its values show sales orders, not plant orders, plus constructed late-order fixtures for V9; **Requested production read-only, always the latest value confirmed into the common plan** at MK × plant × month with its PM breakdown, never a draft or a shared scenario [gate2r12; gate2r9]; the sales Order Book read-only; FMS is never an input. **M1 build [AD]:** contract + channel 1 built; channels 2-3 specified as adapters on the contract and proven with test doubles; real adapters wait for layouts and access (U5, U6) | DS3, DS8, DC10, DC12 | inputs §1, §2, §3; vault; ana050 §3.4; ops-extract; ops tmdl; measured r98; measured r981; gate2r5; gate2r6; gate2r8; gate2r11; gate2r12; gate2r13; gate2r15; gate2r16; gate2r17; gate2r18; critic r3, r4, r7, r8 |

| DF-12 | **Item master: one ITEM table and four dated structures on it** [gate2r16; gate2r17] (T12, T13). (1) **ITEM** — one row per item: origin and element ID (DF-04), caption, **classification** {planning model, finished good / MK, semi-finished, component, **phantom** = SAP phantom assembly: in the BOM, not stocked, its components passing through to the parent}, member type (leaf / "(E)" consolidation), the product hierarchy (division / category / class / product line …) filled for planning models and the manufacturing hierarchy (product line > manufacturing category) for MKs and semi-finished keys, descriptive attributes (e.g. Targeted Plant, product `Plant`, the planning model's **Assembly Cluster** read by the sales cluster filter [gate2r17 OQ-29]), valid from / to. **A planning model and an MK are never one row:** they are two items joined by a bridge link, because one row breaks on N:1 (YCRANE: 7 MKs, 24 PMs, 24 links), 1:N, one-sided IDs (193 demand-only, 181 supply-only), semi-finished keys and components [gate2r16; vault]. (2) **ITEM × PLANT**, dated — the plants at which an MK or semi-finished item is produced (its existence replaces L-03 and `production_eligibility`; no region scope, no priority) and its plant-specific attributes: **default plant share** (CP-07 tier 4, governing the split when the item has more than one row), **cluster** — one concept with the Assembly Cluster, seeded from the MK ID's Manufacturing Category (per suffixed ID for a folded item) [gate2r18 MD-Q-1; seed AD] —, Average Assembly Hours (NW-06; hour capacity itself sits at plant × cluster [gate2r18 MD-Q-16]), week-slot parameters and slot-equivalence factor, frozen-zone length (CP-08), other MK-specific attributes. (3) **ITEM × REGION**, dated — per planning model × region: Region Model Assignment (active, phase-in month, phase-out month) and SOP / EOP; it replaces `item.region`, `item.is_active`, L-06 and P-01 (CP-06). (4) **ITEM × REGION × PLANT — the regional sourcing rule** [gate2r17], dated — per MK × region (L-2) × plant: allowed, priority (1 = preferred), optional default share (NW-08, T13). (5) **ITEM LINKS**, typed and dated, one mechanism for every relation between items: **bridge** PM → MK with percentage weights covering every bridge case (NW-01; no plant qualifier since 9.9.2), **semi-finished** MK → semi-finished with a quantity per parent unit, an offset and a finishing-plant qualifier — the only carrier of a two-step, `-BA` included (NW-03), **routing option** (targeting an ITEM × PLANT row of the same MK by plant ID) and **component option** links with percentage rates, grouped into the **Planning BOM**'s routing and component groups with rate sets per MK × region and global (NW-07), **phantom explosion** phantom → children with a quantity per parent unit. **Plant-suffixed keys** (e.g. `CIT-11-01-000_Oakridge` / `_Northfield`) are **one item with one ITEM × PLANT row per suffix plant**; their dEPM IDs are kept as aliases of those rows for fixtures and the dEPM handoff file (AC24) [gate2r16]. **Plant-suffixed and `-BR` planning models** are merged into one planning-model item per stem, each merged dEPM ID an alias of (planning model, plant) (T12) [gate2r17; gate2r19; merge rule AD]; `-BA` planning models stay their own items, sourced at Northfield [gate2r19]. A phantom has no ITEM × PLANT or ITEM × REGION row, no plan row and no stock. Content seeded per DF-04 and maintained per DF-11; phantom content missing (U9) | DS10, DC13, DC14, DD5, DD11, DS13, DD13 | gate2r16; gate2r17; gate2r18; schema; md; md-spec; vault; xlsx; critic r10 |
| DF-13 | **Full calendar in M1** [gate2r18 MD-Q-12]: one calendar master at **day** grain with **ISO 8601 calendar week** (CW01-CW53; CW53 only in years that ISO 8601 gives 53 weeks, e.g. 2020 and 2026 by that rule; no empty CW53 element in other years [AD]), **month** and **year** (FY = calendar year [gate2r12]), and the week → month assignment (a week belongs to the month holding its Thursday [AD]). Generated for the history window and the horizon, maintained by the data manager (a reassigned week, logged and dated) [AD]. Read by the month buckets and the horizon (CP-01), the Operations Orderbook order dates, day → month (CP-08), the frozen-zone end (CP-08), installation week-slots counted in weeks (NW-06), logs and the handoff months. Working Days stay an Ops input per plant × line key × month (DF-10); per-plant holiday calendars are not in M1 [AD].
