## Plan

Phases 1-4 keep their numbers; new phases 5-12 replace old 5-16. Before Phase 5, GSD (a) records an ADR-001 amendment: R-001 Step B item 1 and Step A into M1, items 2-3 M2 target; the confirm / monthly-publish split, the common plan and the publish under the official names with identity separate from the name (SC-07, DF-03, PR-01) [gate2r7; gate2r9]; authenticated multi-reader / single-writer M1 on a Windows app with server-side scopes, web app in M2 (UX-02, UX-03) [gate2r10]; the two-FY horizon with FY = calendar year (CP-01) [gate2r11; gate2r12]; the frozen zone as default-plus-exception on MK × plant shares (CP-08) [gate2r12; critic r7]; D-001 clarified (Basic Assemblies consume production slots and hours like every key; week-slots only on CIT keys) [user slots], closing the vault open point and SPEC T7 (DM6); the DWH-out-of-runtime rule (DF-01) [gate2r5]; M1 = pilot and the three-channel input rule (DF-10) [gate2r6]; the Planning BOM with the elective routing as its routing group and the CP-07 precedence (NW-07, NW-04; D-006's attribute set becomes the routing group) [gate2r14; AD orchestrator, gate2r14]; the item master in five structures, with plant-suffixed keys folded, plant-suffixed and `-BR` planning models merged, `-BA` kept, dEPM IDs as aliases, multi-plant splits on ITEM × PLANT shares, the regional sourcing rule between the routing group and those shares, and the two-step as a plant-qualified semi-finished link (DF-12, CP-07, NW-03, NW-08; D-002's `_Plant`, `-BA` and `-BR` naming becomes alias, sourcing and link rules) [gate2r16; gate2r17; AD orchestrator, gate2r16]; the global supply planner role, the Product Division scope and role switching (UX-03) [gate2r18]; (b) archives the planned Phase 6 (`.planning/phases/IBP-06-*`, `ANY_SCOPE`), **discarded**; (c) takes in the master-data specification `docs/spec/SPEC-ibp-m1-master-data.md` (DF-11) as an input to Phase 5 wave 1. The evidence base `digests/master-data.md` exists [md]; **the spec written from it does not exist yet** — if it is late, Phase 5 wave 1 builds the schema on DF-02 / DF-04 and the measured dimension inventory [md] and reconciles with the spec before wave 2. (The spec has since been written [md-spec]; from 9.8 it must also carry the component dimension and the Planning BOM link next to L-02 and L-03 — routing and component groups, options, conditions, dated rates per group — an update by its owner, before Phase 7. **From 9.9 it must be realigned to the item master (DF-11, DM9), and from 9.9.2 to the [gate2r17] / [gate2r18] answers listed in DF-11, before Phase 5 wave 1**, because wave 1 builds those tables; if the realignment is late, wave 1 builds on DF-12 and T12 and reconciles with the spec before wave 2.) (d) **rewrites at the stage-6 ingest** the superseded texts in `.planning/REQUIREMENTS.md` and `.planning/ROADMAP.md` [req]: AC5 → "one MK may be produced at several plants; a plant outside the list is accepted and flagged, never rejected" (CP-07), GRANA-01 → its grain clause kept as CP-06, its AC5 precondition removed; AC6 → load in both plants checked in Phase 6, capacity in both plants in Phase 7; AC7 → tested on a routing-group rate of a real-data MK (NW-04 / NW-07).

**Identity refactor — Phase 5, wave 1 [AD].** The Phase 1 amendment "name no longer unique" is executed in Phase 5, not later, because Phase 5 already delivers DF-03 and lands official-name fixtures; Phase 9 only adds publish and supersede on top. Scope: `db/schema.sql:117` `version_name … UNIQUE` → unique on the DF-03 identity, name kept as a non-unique alias; name-keyed lookups move to identity in `db/seed/facts.py:28`, `db/build_db.py:263`, `ibp/scenario/overlay.py:41-44` [critic r5] and the other `version_name` readers (`db/seed/dimensions.py`, `db/seed/scenario.py`, `ibp/scenario/errors.py`) [code r9.3]; `ibp/scenario/models.py` snapshot labels `SNAP_{version_name}_{iso_ts}` carry the identity, not the name, so two same-named versions get distinct snapshots [critic r6]; `ibp/scenario/promote.py:300-301, :334-335` duplicate-name guard → duplicate-identity guard; tests per RG-01.

**Item-master refactor — Phase 5, wave 1, with the identity refactor [AD; gate2r16; gate2r17; gate2r18].** The current schema is already close to the decided model (A3): one `item` table with a role, dated aliases, typed dated links. The deltas, which close DC13 and DC14:

One monthly validity, `period` dropped [AD]. Plant columns: finishing-plant qualifier and supplying plant (semi-finished), target plant ID (routing option); the bridge carries none. | `routing` (`variant_key`, `step_no`, `mandatory`) | mandatory steps → semi-finished links with finishing and supplying plants + ITEM × PLANT rows (6); elective variants → routing-option links to ITEM × PLANT rows (7); the elective two-step variant (NAM, Northfield → Niagara) → a routing option to the finishing plant plus the semi-finished link qualified on it, never a step list (7); table dropped | 6, 7 |
| `bill_of_resource` (MK × `variant_key`) | its `variant_key` goes with `routing`: re-keyed on ITEM × PLANT or dropped when NW-06 is built [AD] | 7 |
| — (none) | option group and rate set tables for the Planning BOM (spec L-11, L-14) on ITEM LINKS; phantom cycle check over overlapping validities | 7, last wave |
| `edge` (generic typed dated edges) | no item-to-item relation any more; keeps only non-item relations (e.g. plant → region) [AD] | 5, wave 1 |
| `TSOP_OPS` keyed `manufact_key` × `plant_key` | unchanged: it is the ITEM × PLANT key; fixtures resolve dEPM IDs through the aliases | — |
| `DPLANT.plant_region`, one value per plant | kept as the descriptive plant region; the scope assignment plant → region (spec L-05, N : M) is separate | 8 |

Order inside wave 1: identity refactor, then the item-master tables, the fold and the merge, the calendar, then fixture conversion — the fixtures resolve dEPM IDs through the new aliases, so fold and merge must exist before any fact lands; the default plant shares are seeded after the Confirmed history, the sourcing rules after the Requested history is in the training store. A grep lists every reader of `item.region`, `item.is_active`, `production_eligibility` and `routing`; their tests follow RG-01. Phase 6 fills every bridge class and the semi-finished links; Phase 7 adds the Planning BOM groups, rate sets and phantom links in its last wave; Phase 12 builds the maintenance masks (sourcing, calendar, FX, Order Intake level, bundles included) and S-04.

**Access control placement [AD].** The engine side of UX-03 (principal on every read and write, active role and role switch, read scopes incl. Product Division and write scopes, view-only enforcement, scope-aware roll-ups, test accounts for the nine roles) lands in Phase 8 with the visibility levels, because both are filters in the same read path. Login in the Windows app, the tool server process with API-01, multi-reader sessions and the identity source (U7) land in Phase 12. The critical path keeps its order (5 → 6 → 7 → 9 → 12); Phase 12 grows by the server and login work, so M1 gets longer by that amount *(inference)*; Phase 8 grows too, but runs in parallel with 6 → 7.

**Frozen-zone checks placement [AD; critic r7 note].** Phase 7 builds the frozen shares, the default and the exception edit, and checks only what needs no confirm or rebase: the default in the common plan, an order-book landing (a fact, no confirm), and an exception edit with reason inside a scenario. What needs confirm or rebase — an exception surviving a later landing in an open scenario, the agenda flag and the PR-06 alert after confirm — is checked in Phase 9, which already depends on Phase 8. This keeps Phase 7 off Phase 8 and the critical path unchanged.

**AC6 placement [gate2r14; AD].** AC6 asks that the mandatory routing consume capacity in **both** plants [req]. The routing and its dependent demand (NW-03) are built in Phase 6; the capacity nodes (NW-06) only in Phase 7. So "capacity consumed" cannot be asserted inside Phase 6. Phase 6's exit check asserts what capacity will consume — for one finished request at Cerritos, load at MK × plant × month booked at Ganderkesee (semi-finished, two months earlier) **and** at Cerritos (finishing); Phase 7's exit check reruns the same fixture and asserts hours and production slots consumed at **both** Ganderkesee and Cerritos. AC6 closes in Phase 7. Pulling NW-06 into Phase 6 for one check would move the capacity inputs and their seed forward and lengthen the critical path; not done. Split confirmed by the user [gate2r15].

**Planning BOM placement [gate2r14; AD].** Phase 7 builds the Planning BOM (NW-07, with NW-04 inside it) as its own last wave, because it reads the requested load at MK × plant × region (Phase 5), the bridge weights and the semi-finished dependent demand (Phase 6), the plant split it shares with CP-07 and the allocation proposal, and the order-book horizon (Phase 7); the routing group replaces the NW-04 work Phase 7 already held. Nothing else in M1 reads the component requirement, so the wave does not hold up Phase 9's checks; it does lengthen Phase 7, and with it the critical path *(inference)*. If U9 has not delivered, Phase 7 closes on constructed fixtures and the real content is loaded later as master data through its maintenance mask; the rate proposal and the ordered-then-remainder rule are proven with a test-double configured Order Book until U1 delivers. The phantom pass-through (PB9) is part of the same wave and is proven on constructed phantoms. Its maintenance mask and the chain view are UX-spec items; the chain view's engine query is printed by the CLI in Phase 7 and shown in the app in Phase 12.

**Named risks.** (1) Slice 1 is large: real-size perimeter, the input layer, the identity refactor of the Phase 1-4 code (schema, lookups, promote guard, snapshot labels, AC13 test) and now the item-master refactor. Mitigation: wave 1 = identity refactor + item-master tables and fold + stores + fixture conversion + set-based build + pair count + latency (CP-05); the refactors run first so the 187 tests are green on identity and on the new item tables before any new node lands. (2) Pilot, not live [gate2r6]: M1 runs on real history loaded as fixtures through the input layer; nobody keys data twice. The pilot proves the concurrent loop, inventory row, back-allocation, capacity, the frozen-share default, scenarios, cross-function visibility between authenticated role sessions, scoped view-only access, the monthly review at real size, the stored-value reproductions, CP-05 latency, the landing contract (channel 1 plus test doubles) and the handoff-file layout. It does not prove planner adoption under a real cycle, simultaneous writers, real ERP / Snowflake adapters and their data quality (U5, U6), dEPM import of the handoff file (U2), the Planning BOM on real component data (U9), or value on live decisions. Double entry beside dEPM becomes an M2 concern (T10). (3) Openings: the inventory row is empty until the m−1 openings land. Mitigation: openings through any DF-10 channel at PM (fixtures in the pilot), a coarser channel 2-3 arrival spread on the cycle's plan Sales / Invoices (CP-02), missing ones counted and flagged and on the PR-05 agenda; never a DWH copy at runtime. The spread misplaces stock between models when stock does not follow the plan; Phase 5 measures that error against stored inventory and reports it. (4) Hour capacity has no documented input mask; it is master data seeded from history [AD]. (5) Accuracy QC and back-tests depend on DWH history; if the extracts stop, the control stops, not the planning loop. (6) Two objects named `CYCLE MM YYYY R` (loaded reference, tool-published): a name-keyed lookup would silently keep one (e.g. the `facts.py` dict [critic r5]). Mitigation: the identity refactor in Phase 5 wave 1, a grep check that no lookup by `version_name` remains, and origin + sequence shown next to the name in every view and file. (7) The production alert depends on the bridge: large bridge groups (N:1 YCRANE, multi-plant keys) blur which PM or MK caused a gap, and unmatched IDs fall outside it; summing regions to net intra-group flows hides a regional cause. Mitigation: alerts show the PM, MK and region breakdown; Phase 6 prints group sizes, the volume outside the alert, the cross-region and cross-group intra-group volumes and the in-transit residual. (8) FMS on uncovered cells: dEPM's horizon rule is unknown, so ~20 % of cells (not covered within the plan) will differ from stored values, more near the FY-end edge in late-year cycles. Mitigation: [AD] default flagged "not covered", the gap reported in Phase 5; the rule text can come from Infor or the S&OP data manager if the user wants exact parity. (9) Continuous work beside a dEPM that still runs its day calendar in M1: the tool's monthly publish and dEPM's own P/R/A/C can diverge, and ≥ 1-unit alerts may flood the other role. Mitigation: the handoff file carries R and C (and P / A copies if needed); Phase 9 counts alerts per replayed month and sets thresholds from that count. (10) Access control in M1: a server process on ACMELIFT infrastructure, an unknown identity source and a DuckDB store that allows one writing process. Mitigation: one server process owns the store and serializes writes, readers go through API-01; tool-local accounts if U7 is not answered; a scope leak test (every role, every read endpoint, compare and KPIs included) in Phases 8 and 12. (11) A December horizon of 13 months is shorter than what dEPM stored (`CYCLE 12 2025 P` runs to 2027-12 [fms]); the handoff file then carries fewer months than dEPM may expect. FY = calendar year is confirmed [gate2r12], so the rule stands. Mitigation: Phase 9 replays a December cycle and prints the month count in the handoff. (12) Frozen-share default without any Operations Orderbook history: no source holds the assembly plants' own sales orders — the ops cube `OrderBook_units` is a copy of the sales order book [gate2r18] — so the pilot runs the default on a proxy whose values are sales orders distributed to plants *(inference until the U10 export)*, not plant orders; real values arrive only by ops-planner entry (live use, M2) or an ERP extract of the plants' sales-order lines (U5). If order lines carry no LE / PM, the allocation can put a late order's units on the wrong entity. The split basis can age: a cell frozen for months keeps the MK × plant split the planner last entered before the default, and a cell never entered falls back to the CP-07 split × link weights, which may not match today's order book [critic r8]. Mitigation: proxy fixtures labelled "proxy: sales order book"; constructed late-order fixtures for the V9 mechanics; Phase 7 prints the proxy's totals vs the sales Order Book (equal by construction, a load check), the share of frozen units allocated rather than keyed, the count of cells split on each basis and the count of cells with mixed frozen and open shares. (13) Opening double count: the UM's IC includes plant stock to be invoiced to the entity [inputs §2]; if a planner keeps typing that part in IC while plants also report FGNI, the opening is overstated. Mitigation: the decided rule (IC excludes plant-held units) [gate2r13], the S-06 info pop-up and the "i" texts of both fields; Phase 6 prints Δ inventory with and without FGNI (DF-09). (14) The Planning BOM has no data: no source names a component, rate or phantom (A7), so M1 can prove the mechanism only on constructed fixtures, and the 150 configurable-suffix keys are a naming rule, not a verified list. Mitigation: U9 asks for the content and owner; Phase 7 counts MKs with and without a Planning BOM; rates are master data, so loading them later needs no build. (15) Phase 7 carries capacity, allocation, the frozen zone and now the Planning BOM, on the critical path. Mitigation: the Planning BOM is the last wave and nothing downstream waits for its real content; if Phase 7 must be cut, that wave can move after Phase 9 without changing any other phase *(inference)*. (16) A routing group overrides the master split (CP-07): once rates are loaded, the plant split of a routing MK can move away from its history-seeded shares and shift load between plants. Mitigation: Phase 7 prints, per routing MK, the load by master split vs by routing group, and the routing rates are dated master data the data manager can correct. (17) The fold and the merge change keys under history and the handoff (MK IDs folded, plant-suffixed and `-BR` planning-model IDs merged [gate2r17; gate2r19]): a suffix that is not a plant (a false match of the fold rule), a fact row on a suffixed ID at another plant, or a plant reached by the tool for which dEPM has no ID would break the round trip into dEPM. Mitigation: the fold rule requires the suffix to resolve to a plant; Phase 5 lists every fold (stem, IDs, plants) for the data manager, counts rows landed with "suffix plant ≠ fact plant", and every merge (stem, IDs, alias plants), and asserts that the dry-run handoff returns every unedited fixture cell under the dEPM IDs and values it landed with; rows without a dEPM ID are listed, never renamed; merged planning models lose per-alias FMS parity with dEPM, listed. (18) Default plant shares of multi-plant items, folded or not, come from 12 closed months of Confirmed history: a key whose plant mix has changed recently starts from a stale split, and a key without history lands on the product `Plant` plant or UNASSIGNED. Mitigation: shares are dated master data with their seed window, basis (history / product `Plant` / none) and UNASSIGNED volume printed per item in Phase 5. (19) The `-BA` mapping is not in the evidence (T12): if stems, base MKs or `-BA` keys do not pair by the suffix rule, `-BA` demand stays demand-only and basic-assembly load at Northfield is missed; if the 28 regex matches are not the basic assemblies [critic r11], semi-finished links would be built on wrong items. Mitigation: the Phase 5 counts and lists, the check of the 3 Basic Assemblies against the 28, the data manager's confirmation of the 28 before Phase 6 links them, hand-made dated links for unpaired ones, their volume on the PR-05 agenda. (20) Sourcing rules are sparse at start: only the merged IDs' history seeds them; every other MK × region follows the item-wide shares, which carry no regional preference, until the global supply planner enters rules — the LATAM and APAC preferences of [gate2r17] are in no source. Mitigation: Phase 5 prints MK × region pairs with demand and no rule, by volume; the PR-05 agenda lists the largest; rules are dated master data loadable by file. (21) Region at L-2 [gate2r18]: history rows on members below L-2 are summed at landing, and the handoff writes L-2 node IDs, which dEPM may not accept as input (U2 unverified). Mitigation: Phase 5 counts the rolled-up rows; the dry-run handoff report lists every region ID it writes. (22) Value gap without data: no price and no FX series is on disk, so the value gap stays blank until the data manager enters the first prices (price table, gate2r19) and FX rates. Mitigation: blank cells counted, the units gap always shown. (23) A new role and co-editing (global supply planner, Operations on capacity and rates) widen who changes master data that moves load. Mitigation: every master-data change is dated and logged, moves of requested load alert ops (PR-06), and write rights are tested per role in Phases 8 and 12.

Gate 1: G1 over-forecast is an error, no margin · G2 binding lags 3-6 · G3 AC21/AC22 in M1 · G4 C0 = 2022-01 · G5 actuals on the 3rd working day.

### Still open: 2 (readings of [gate2r19] to confirm; each with an [AD] default; none blocks the M1 pilot)

- **OQ-35 — `-BA` and the two-step.** You said the `-BA` models stay for Northfield and the base model's NAM demand goes to Niagara. **Reading [AD]:** the `-BA` planning model is a model of its own built at Northfield; the semi-finished link of MD-Q-20 (the base MK finished at Niagara books a basic assembly at Northfield two months earlier) is kept. Change it if the `-BA` model *is* the Northfield basic assembly and no separate link is wanted. → B7, NW-03, T12.
- **OQ-36 — Twin plants.** **Reading [AD]:** twin pairs are dated master data (Northfield ↔ Niagara first; others entered by the global supply planner or the data manager); the automatic re-split runs on every order-book landing, moves only ordered units whose order line names a plant, keeps the cell total, and inside the frozen zone follows the frozen share. Which other twin pairs exist (for example a twin of Pinecrest do Sul)? → NW-08, CP-07, CP-08.

| Tool-published versions exported to Power BI | not in M1 | Power BI reads the DWH; the official version reaches it through dEPM [pbi; AC24] |

| Day windows (sales 1-8, ops 10-15, allocation 15, validation 16) as gates in the tool; P and A as working instances | never in the tool | one monthly review, continuous work [gate2r9]; R ≈ P, A = C (V1, V2); dEPM keeps its own calendar outside it in M1 |
| A rolling 24-month horizon | never | the horizon is the rest of the current FY plus the next FY [gate2r11] |
| Frozen zone as a lock that hides or blocks Requested; a frozen zone applied to a whole planning-model cell; the defaulted load used as its own split basis | never | the zone stays visible; only frozen MK × plant shares follow the Operations Orderbook, a change of a frozen share is an exception with reason; the split comes from the pre-default request or the CP-07 split [gate2r12; critic r7, r8] |
| Total invoiced (Own Region and Group included) as stock depletion in the inventory row or in the DF-09 alert | never | intra-group invoices do not leave the group; they would create false implied production; one stock identity with CP-02 [vault; critic r7] |

New defects DS12, DS13, DC15, DD12, DD13; DS8, DD4, DD11, DM9 rewritten; new cases S11, N5, V11, P12. **Requirements:** new CP-09, DF-13, DF-14, NW-08; changed CP-01, CP-02, CP-03, CP-06, CP-07, CP-08, SC-02, SC-07, DF-02, DF-04, DF-05, DF-10, DF-11, DF-12, NW-01, NW-02, NW-03, NW-05, NW-06, NW-07, PR-04, PR-05, PR-06, QC-02, UX-01, UX-03, UX-05. Revision 9.9.1 (critic on 9.9: FAIL, two blocking findings and five notes [critic r10]; the fixes are rules decided by the orchestrator on the user's behalf [AD orchestrator, gate2r16]). **Blocking 1 — the product `Plant` attribute sent multi-plant keys to one plant (fixed).** CP-07 tier 3 had put a bridge plant qualifier seeded from the single-valued product `Plant` attribute ahead of the ITEM × PLANT shares, so B5 `Crane-Tipper (S)` → folded `CIT-11-01-000` and the 10 two-plant P1 keys sent all units to one plant. Now: an MK with more than one ITEM × PLANT row splits by its default plant shares and the product `Plant` attribute is not read; it is read only for a single-plant MK and as the seed fallback. Seed for folded and non-folded multi-plant items alike: Confirmed units per plant over the last 12 closed months (suffix plant for a folded ID, fact plant otherwise); no history → 100 % on the product `Plant` plant if it is a row, else UNASSIGNED on the PR-05 agenda. The calibrated-mix tier is absorbed into these shares. Precedence: override > demand-side qualifier > routing group > shares / single row > UNASSIGNED. The demand-side qualifier (a planning model's own plant suffix, B6, or the `-BA` finishing plant, B7) is the architect's addition to the orchestrator's order [AD], kept because without it B6's suffix and B7's finishing plant would be lost; it is never seeded from the product `Plant` attribute. B5, P1, P7 re-walked. Changed: CP-07, CP-08 (split basis), NW-01, NW-02, NW-05, DF-01, DF-11, DF-12, T1, T2.4, T3, T4, T12, the walk table (B1, B4, B5, B6, P1, P7), Plan (delta table), risks (16), (18), Phase 5 and 7 exit checks, a new exclusion. **Blocking 2 — the `-BA` two-step had two carriers and fired at every plant (fixed).** The two-step now has one carrier, the semi-finished link (NW-03), removed from the routing-group options (NW-04, NW-07, T12, PB6). The semi-finished link carries a finishing-plant qualifier and a supplying ITEM × PLANT row and fires only on the parent's load at the finishing plant (Cerritos → Ganderkesee; Niagara → Northfield, offset 2), never at other plants. The 28 `-BA` MK IDs of the MK dimension [md] are seeded as semi-finished items (DF-04), no longer "created by the data manager". The 21 `-BA` planning models bridge to their base MK (suffix removed, exact ID match [AD]) with the finishing plant as qualifier, and the base MK carries the semi-finished link to the `-BA` item. The evidence does not map the 21 to the 28, nor say whether each base MK exists: Phase 5 counts and lists the paired, demand-only and supply-only `-BA` keys (B7, B9, T12), new risk (19). P8 and P9 re-walked; B8 gains the 13 `-BR` MK IDs [md]. **Notes.** (a) Quantity semantics: percentages (weights, rates, attach rates, 0 % allowed) and quantities per parent unit (semi-finished, phantom explosion, > 0, unbounded) are separate columns with one CHECK per link type (Plan delta table, NW-07, T12, DC14). (b) A routing option targets an ITEM × PLANT row of the same MK, named by plant ID (NW-07, T12, PB6). (c) The fold's match on the 62 CIT keys and 13 leaves is marked as inference, counted in Phase 5 (T12). (d) PB7 states the phantom exception to "an option is never exploded further". (e) The phantom cycle check runs over every combination of links whose validities overlap (T12, PB9, Phase 7). **Requirements moved:** CP-07, CP-08, DF-01, DF-04, DF-11, DF-12, NW-01, NW-02, NW-03, NW-04, NW-05, NW-07, PR-05, UX-04, UX-05 (split wording); none new. Revision 9.8.1 (critic on 9.8: FAIL, three blocking findings and six notes [critic r9]; the fixes are rules decided by the orchestrator on the user's behalf [AD orchestrator, gate2r14]; evidence re-measured [measured r981]). **Blocking 1 — routing rates applied twice (fixed).** NW-07 now has two kinds of group: a routing group (at most one per MK, its options carry a plant) and component groups. The routing group splits the MK load across plants once, and the load of a routing option at its plant is the units routed there, never multiplied by the rate again. Component groups apply to the load at each plant; a component group may be conditioned on a routing option, otherwise its rate applies uniformly. A routing option is a component only if declared so. The critic's example is now a Phase 7 exit-check case: red → X 30 %, blue → Y 70 %, 100 units gives red 30 at X and 0 at Y. Changed: NW-07, NW-04, T1, T2.13, T4, T6, PB1, PB6, a new exclusion. **Blocking 2 — CP-07 tier order (fixed).** CP-07 now states its precedence, highest first: planner override, routing group, master attribute `Plant` / `ManufactKey`, calibrated mix, `UNASSIGNED`. The routing group overrides the master attribute; the master attribute stays the default for MKs without a routing group and for units with no routing rate in force. AC7 is tested on a real-data MK: "LC 10000" appears in the captions of 6 MK dimension rows [measured r981], with a P1 multi-plant key as fallback. Changed: CP-07, T2.4, NW-05, P7, P9, risk (16), Plan (a)/(d), the Phase 7 exit check. **Blocking 3 — order configuration wins (fixed).** The configuration source is the sales Order Book with order-line configuration (U1 feed, LE × region × PM × month); the order-book horizon is per cell — the months in which that feed carries ordered units — with no global constant. One rule now covers both the plant split and the components: ordered units go by their real configuration, the remainder max(0, requested − ordered) by rates, and ordered > requested means ordered governs and the cell is flagged. Matching is per cell, mapped to MK through the bridge weights. Example: 40 at Niagara + 30 % × 60 = 58. The test double has the same shape. Changed: NW-07, CP-07, DF-10, T3 (new row), T4, PB5, U1, the Phase 7 exit check. **Notes (fixed).** (a) S9, A1b: first Order Intake plan rows in cycles 08-09/2025, none on `CYCLE 07 2025` [measured r981]; DC12 and T3 read 08/2025. (b) The extract labels all 143,019 `OrderIntake_units` rows "Order Intake [external]", cited for the OQ-27 default and in DF-10. (c) Phase 5 states how the 2,130 leaf-product Order Intake rows land: mapped to their PM directly through the `DSALES_Product` ↔ PM mapping, not spread. (d) The regional fallback is per whole group: a region rate set replaces the whole group and still sums to 100 % (NW-07, PB3). (e) DF-09 now separates the typed Invoiced Total (OQ-26) from the calculated inventory Total = Financial + IC. (f) T1 hangs the Planning BOM off the finished MK load at each plant, not only off the semi-finished load. **AC6:** capacity in both plants stays in Phase 7, with Phase 6 testing load in both plants (AC6 placement, NW-03, P8); this split is **pending the user's confirmation**. **Requirements moved:** CP-07, DF-09, DF-10, DF-11, NW-03 (note only), NW-04, NW-05, NW-07, UX-04; none other. Counts unchanged: 84 cases, 54 requirements, 1 open question (OQ-27).

The exclusion "order-intake forecasting not in M1" now names statistical forecasting only. **(2) [gate2r14] Planning BOM.** New **NW-07** (closes the new defect DS9), new case group PB1-PB8, new as-is A7 (150 of 1,365 MK IDs with configurable suffixes [measured r98]; no source names a component or rate). **NW-04 is unified into NW-07** (row kept as a pointer; AC7 tested on an option rate). Revision 9.7.1 (critic round 2 on 9.7: PASS [critic r8]; five non-blocking notes, no scope change). **(1) Non-circular split basis.** Inside a zone the requested load is itself the order-book default, so it cannot be the basis that splits a planning-model cell into MK × plant shares. CP-08 now splits on the cell's pre-default request (kept per share) where it exists, else on the CP-07 plant mix × the bridge link weights [AD]; the order-book allocation to cells uses the same basis. Rule, decided by the orchestrator on the user's behalf: DF-09 uses CP-02's identity (Δ inventory + External invoiced), Own Region and Group are intra-group [AD] and net out because every LE of the bridge group is summed; the alert grain becomes bridge group × month with region summed by default [AD], region breakdown shown; Total is for display and import only. Rule, decided by the orchestrator on the user's behalf: the cell is split into MK × plant shares by the request share (NW-02 weights in reverse); only shares of pairs inside their **own** zone (own length, own Working Days) take the Operations Orderbook; other shares keep the planner's request; an edit of the total spreads over open shares [AD], and is an exception only if it changes a frozen share; UNASSIGNED plant → no default, cell on the agenda [AD]; P11 follows the supplying plant [AD]. Contradictions with the evidence, stated, not smoothed: "targets in the planning mask" [gate2 OQ-9] vs no target [gate2r3 OQ-B']; opening "finance inventory / Snowflake / ERP" [gate2r3 OQ-G'] vs "manual input" [gate2r4] vs "DWH only for training" [gate2r5] — reconciled by [gate2r6]: mask entry, ERP extract and Snowflake are all operational channels, the DWH is not; "no pilot" [user, perimeter] vs "M1 = pilot" [gate2r6, usage mode] — Scope 3 reworded; confirm into a new user-named version (revisions 5-9.1) vs official name [gate2r2 OQ-A; gate2r7] — official name applied; "a confirmed scenario leaves as `CYCLE MM YYYY R`" [gate2r7] vs confirm = commit into the common plan, publish = monthly freeze [gate2r9] — reconciled: the published month leaves, carrying every confirmed change; the documented day calendar [inputs §4], the 9.4 PR-01 and "keep P and R" [gate2 OQ-2] vs continuous work and one monthly review [gate2r9] — user decision applied in the tool, dEPM keeps its calendar outside it in M1, P / A survive as history and handoff copies [AD]; "M1 single-user next to dEPM" [gate2r2 OQ-E; Scope 7 before 9.5] and "offline on Windows" (AC25) vs authenticated multi-user read access in M1 [gate2r10] — resolved: single writer, many authenticated readers, one tool server on ACMELIFT Windows infrastructure, single-PC mode still offline; web app in M2; "24 months" planning window (UM, DF-10 before 9.5) vs two fiscal years, 13 months in December [gate2r11] — user applied; the stored `CYCLE 12 2025 P` already runs to 2027-12 [fms] and January cycles in history lacked the next year at lag 12 (E13) — both recorded; FY = calendar year assumed by the vault, not stated [vault] — confirmed by the user [gate2r12]; Invoiced / Order Intake with three columns in the UM vs four categories incl. Total [gate2r11] — Total typed [gate2r13] (the 9.5-9.7.1 default "calculated" reversed), its difference to the sum flagged [AD]; DF-09 "invoiced = Total" (9.5-9.6) vs the inventory row's external-only depletion (CP-02) and the vault's "only third-party (external) invoiced units", intercompany "netted out" [vault] — external depletion applied in both, intra-group netted at bridge-group level [critic r7, orchestrator decision]; UM Order Intake column descriptions mislabelled [inputs] — category meaning for the "i" texts to be checked by the UX designer; "S-06 removed, opening entered in S-05" (thread b536c5cf, first answer) vs S-06 kept as the opening-inventory mask [gate2r12] — later decision applied; "Total = Financial + IC is what feeds the import and the calculation" vs "FG in assembly plants not invoiced enters the calculation" [both gate2r12] — read as: Total is what an import carries, the calculation uses Total + FGNI [AD]; the UM's IC includes plant stock to be invoiced to the entity [inputs §2] vs a separate FGNI figure [gate2r12] — IC excludes plant-held units from M1 on [gate2r13]; the UM's Ops mask shows the Order Book read-only from the Sales cycle [inputs §1] vs the ops planner sees and enters an Operations Orderbook [gate2r12] — user decision applied; FMS "-" = "total FMS without division" [gate2r3 OQ-B'] vs model × region roll-up over divisions in the stored data [fms] — data applied, the user's "same logic" kept; Power BI FMS DAX vs dEPM stored logic [fms] — dEPM applied; "condensed BOM" (the user's comment) vs "Planning BOM" [gate2r14] — the user's chosen name used throughout; "one element table with everything" (the user's question) vs normalised links — answered [gate2r14]: no wide storage table, one computed read view (UX-04) — and then [gate2r16]: one ITEM table for identity and classification, with links, plants and regions in their own dated structures, so the two answers hold together; Product Division not a scope in M1 (9.7 default) vs needed [gate2r18] — added as a read scope; "calendar-week map not in M1" (spec default) vs a full calendar [gate2r18] — calendar applied, the dEPM map itself not reproduced.

## Phase briefs

Each brief is self-sufficient: load it plus the sections under "Read".

### Phase 5 — Walking skeleton: the concurrent loop at real size, driven by inputs
- **Goal:** real history is loaded into the training / verification store and converted into mask-layout input files that act as **seed / test fixtures** of the M1 pilot, not as a live source [gate2r6]: cycle 09/2026 sales inputs (the Order Intake forecast from stored `OrderIntake_Units` included) and 08/2026 C ops inputs over the two-FY horizon; m−1 actuals and openings at LE × region × PM and plant × region × MK; the closed current-FY months as actuals. They land through channel 1 on the DF-10 landing contract into the cycle's common plan, keyed on the item master (DF-12). Then: enter the opening (Financial, IC, FGNI), create a personal scenario, edit Sales / Invoices and Requested production for one LE × region × PM × month; see the current-FY actuals beside the plan, the inventory row (requested switch), FMS at the same grain and its roll-ups, requested load at MK × plant × region, and the gap vs confirmed at MK × plant × month; compare with the common plan; write a dry-run handoff file.
- **Delivers:** identity refactor (Plan); **item-master refactor (DF-12; Plan): ITEM with classification incl. component and phantom (the 28 `-BA` MK IDs as semi-finished, pending the critic r11 check), ITEM × PLANT with default plant shares seeded per CP-07 tier 4 and cluster seeded from Manufacturing Category, ITEM × REGION (from U11 or plan-fact pairs), ITEM × REGION × PLANT sourcing rules seeded from the merged aliases (NW-08), ITEM LINKS with separate percentage and quantity columns, bridge links for the 1:1 classes and the folded keys, plant-suffixed MK IDs folded and plant-suffixed / `-BR` planning models merged with plant-scoped dEPM aliases and the per-cell alias split**; the day calendar with ISO weeks (DF-13), FY = calendar year and two-FY horizon (CP-01); region keyed at L-2 (CP-06); the Order Intake level setting (SC-02); schema and dimension seed per the master-data spec (DF-11, realigned to DF-12; if the spec is late, on DF-02 / DF-04 / DF-12 / T12 and the dimension inventory of `master-data.md`, reconciled before wave 2); DF-01…DF-08 (DF-08: Produced Units at plant × region × MK), DF-10 (landing contract + channel 1: file import and CLI entry; grain, type, sign, key and horizon validation live, lock and frozen-zone validation stubbed until Phases 9 and 7; actuals categories with all four typed, Total included; the Order Intake forecast in the sales planning measures; opening columns Financial, IC, Total, FGNI at destination grain), CP-01 (without capacity nodes and frozen-share default), CP-02 (requested side; opening = Total + FGNI; confirmed views fall back to requested, labelled; confirmed side and FGNI plant × MK mapping in 6), CP-03 (requested FMS and roll-ups), CP-04…CP-07 (CP-06 with ITEM × REGION; CP-07 tiers 1 (overrides and alias pins), 3 (sourcing rule), 4 and 5 with AC5 as realigned on ITEM × PLANT; the routing-group tier arrives with NW-07 in Phase 7), SC-01, SC-02 (Order Intake all-zero fallback), SC-03 (create), SC-04, SC-07 (dry-run file under the official names, marked dry-run, dEPM IDs through the aliases), SC-08, RG-01 (AC13 duplicate test rewritten to identity; tests on `item.region`, `production_eligibility` and `routing` moved to the DF-12 lookups).
- **Cases:** S1, S2, S4, S7, S8, S9, N1, N2, N3, N4 (destination grain), E5-E10, E13, E18-E20, E23, E24, V2-V7, B1-B3, B5 (fold), B6 (merge), B7 and B8 (merge and counts), B9, B10, B12, P1, P2, P4-P7, P11, P12 (seeded and constructed rules), V11.
- **Waves:** (0) ADR amendment, archive old Phase 6, take in the master-data spec (realigned to DF-12); (1) identity refactor (schema, lookups, snapshot labels, promote guard, AC13 test), item-master refactor (five structures, fold and merge, aliases and alias split, the reader grep), day calendar, FY and horizon, L-2 region roll-up, two stores, training-store load rules, default plant shares seeded from the Confirmed history, sourcing rules seeded from the Requested history of merged aliases, cluster seed and 78 / 81 reconciliation, hour-capacity seed rule measured, `-BA` / `-BR` counts, fixture conversion, MK → line-key join, set-based build, pair count, latency; (2) input layer (landing contract, channel 1), common plan node, opening with its columns, log and fallback spread, input-grain edits, new DAG nodes (inventory, FMS); (3) compare, handoff file, CLI.
- **Confirmed side:** empty in Phase 5 for every cycle. Stored `Production_units_C` at sales grain does not exist before ~2025 cycles [critic r3] (§3.3 live since 06/2025), so scenarios on older cycles get a confirmed side only when Phase 6 computes it, with no stored reference to verify against.
- **Exit check:** schema unique on identity, not on `version_name`; a grep finds no version lookup by `version_name` outside alias display; two versions sharing one name load, resolve and snapshot separately; AC13 duplicate test rewritten per RG-01, the rest of the suite unchanged and green; a test proves no runtime read from the training store into the engine except through the input layer, calibrated profiles and seeded master data; every landed value carries channel and source provenance, rejects counted per channel; the lock and frozen-zone hooks exist and are reported as stubs. **Item master:** the `item` table has no region column and no `is_active`; a planning model and an MK with the same ID string load as two items; every planning model × region with plan facts in the last cycle has an active ITEM × REGION row, and a sales row on a pair without one lands, is flagged and counted; `production_eligibility` and `routing` are no longer read; a link row with a percentage above 100 or a semi-finished / phantom link without a positive quantity is rejected, a quantity of 2 is accepted; the fold lists every stem with its dEPM IDs and plants (the count printed; the 62 CIT keys and 13 leaves are the expectation, not a target), `CIT-11-01-000_Oakridge` and `_Northfield` load as one item with two ITEM × PLANT rows, each dEPM fixture ID resolves to exactly one item or ITEM × PLANT row, rows with "suffix plant ≠ fact plant" are counted, and the dry-run handoff returns every fixture dEPM ID unchanged, rows without a dEPM ID listed. **Plant split (CP-07) [critic r10]:** every multi-plant item (folded and non-folded) prints its default shares with their 12-month window and basis (history / product `Plant` / none), the UNASSIGNED volume is counted; a fixture request on `Crane-Tipper (S)` and on one of the 10 two-plant keys lands at both plants by those shares, never all at the product `Plant` plant; a single-plant MK whose product `Plant` differs from its row is flagged. **Merge (T12) [gate2r17]:** every stem with its merged planning-model IDs and alias plants is listed (36 plant-suffixed and 8 `-BR` are the expectation, not a target; the 21 `-BA` stay unmerged); a suffix that does not resolve to a plant is not merged and is listed; a fixture row on `<stem>_<plant>` lands on the stem's cell pinned to that plant; an unedited fixture cell returns in the dry-run handoff under exactly the dEPM IDs and values it landed with, an edited one scales its alias parts in proportion and still sums to the cell; merged planning models are inside the inventory reproduction and listed outside the FMS reproduction. **Sourcing (NW-08):** seeded rules print per MK × region their plants, priorities, shares and 12-month basis; MK × region pairs with demand and no rule are printed by volume; on constructed rules, a LATAM cell (LATAM plant preferred, EMEA allowed, no shares) loads 100 % at the LATAM plant, an APAC cell with shares 50 / 30 / 20 splits so, an EMEA cell without a rule splits by the item-wide shares; an override to an allowed plant carries no flag, to another plant is flagged "outside sourcing rule"; a save with shares ≠ 100 % or two preferred plants is rejected. **Region L-2:** rows on members below L-2 are rolled up, totals preserved, counted. **Calendar:** day grain from history start to the horizon end; ISO weeks with CW53 only in 53-week years; each week in the month of its Thursday. **Cluster:** the 78 Assembly Cluster and 81 Manufacturing Category values listed as in both / only one, PM ↔ MK pairs whose values differ listed; every ITEM × PLANT row carries a cluster or is listed empty; the 73 non-line "(E)" capacity keys matched to a cluster by name, matched / unmatched counted; the `AssemblyCapacity_hours` pattern inside plant × cluster × month (equal leaf values or not) printed and the hour-capacity seed follows it. **ITEM × REGION seed:** source printed (U11 parameters cube or plan-fact pairs). **`-BA` / `-BR` counts (T12) [critic r11]:** whether the 3 Basic Assembly keys of B11 are among the 28 `-BA` IDs — if not, the 28 are listed for the data manager to confirm as semi-finished before Phase 6 creates their links; `-BA` PMs kept, with and without a resolving base MK, `-BA` semi-finished items with and without a pairing base MK, the plant rows of each `-BA` key, the `-BR` PMs and MKs merged into their base with their Pinecrest do Sul rows, and any `-BA` / `-BR` ID also matched by the fold rule, each listed with volume. **Horizon:** a January cycle carries 24 plan months and a December cycle 13; closed current-FY months show actuals and reject edits; an edit beyond the next FY end is rejected. **Actuals:** Invoiced and Order Intake land by External / Own Region / Group / Total as typed; a Total that differs from the sum of the three is accepted, flagged and counted; Total is not used by the inventory row. **Order Intake forecast:** the 09/2026 fixture's rows on "(E)" product members (97.2 % of stored rows) are spread to LE × region × PM on the plan Sales / Invoices of the same months, totals preserved per member × month, provenance "spread"; its rows on leaf products (2.8 %, 2,130 over all stored cycles) are mapped to their PM directly through the `DSALES_Product` ↔ PM mapping, provenance "mapped", and any leaf row without a PM is rejected and counted; an edit of the forecast moves no inventory, FMS, load or gap; it appears in compare and in the dry-run handoff on the "(E)" member. **AC5 as realigned:** a planner override and a landed Confirmed row on a plant outside the MK's ITEM × PLANT rows are accepted, flagged "new key × plant pair" and counted; no typed rejection exists. **Opening:** Total = Financial + IC on every cell; a negative IC is accepted; a negative Financial or FGNI typed by a planner is rejected and counted, a negative Financial from the fixture channel is accepted, flagged "negative fixture" and counted; the row starts from Total + FGNI; FGNI entered at two plants for one destination cell sums into it; a plant without FGNI for m−1 is flagged and counted. AC10 on real-size data; AC29 bound met, times vs CP-05 (if missed: stop before wave 2). Verification: with fixture openings at LE × region × PM equal to stored inventory (as Financial, IC = 0, FGNI = 0), the row reproduces stored `Inventory_units_end` of `CYCLE 09 2026 R` (21,119 rows [critic]) or mismatches are listed. **FMS — comparison window stated:** stored `CYCLE 12 2025 P` spans 30 months (2025-07..2027-12) while the tool's December horizon spans 13 (2025-12..2026-12), so the comparison runs on the 13 tool months only; a cell counts as covered when its window crosses before 2026-12 ends, and on those cells the node reproduces stored `FMSRate_calc` to 1e-6 (the digest's 39,918 / 39,920 was measured over the stored span and is an upper reference, not the target count; the tool-window count is printed); cells covered only by 2027 sales in dEPM are listed as edge cells with the [AD] default vs the stored value, counted per horizon month; the "-" and product-line roll-ups reproduce their stored values on the same window; one 2021-2024 cycle is checked for the same "-" rule; a Sales / Invoices edit in month k moves FMS of earlier months. Opening entries logged, missing openings counted. **Fallback spread test:** an opening fixture at Division × product line (stored inventory summed to that grain, standing in for a coarse channel 2-3 arrival) is spread on the 09/2026 plan Sales / Invoices m..m+2 at LE × region × PM, falling back to Requested; the totals are preserved, the provenance reads "spread", and an all-zero basis is rejected and counted; the spread error against stored full-grain inventory is printed (WMAPE-style, units). Compare refuses mixed switches; DF-05 counts and DF-08 reconciliation printed; sales actuals fixtures land at LE × region × PM, Produced Units fixtures at plant × region × MK and match ops `C_Total` I at plant × MK; load regression checks on capacity history (0 unmapped keys, line key = max of leaves, fallback count, line-key production volume); `DSALES_Product` ↔ PM mapping counted; LN count printed (132 expected [md]); plants without region counted (5 expected [md]); dry-run handoff file passes the layout check; no scenario name reaches Power BI. User check of the file = UAT checklist.
- **Read:** Scope; Cases S, N, E, V, B1-B3, B5, B7-B10, B12, P; As-is A1, A1b, A1c, A2, A2b, A3, DC1-DC14, DD1-DD11, DM8, DM9; Requirements CP, SC, DF (incl. DF-11, DF-12), RG; To-be T1-T5 (incl. "Channels in M1"), T9, T12; Plan (identity refactor, item-master refactor, step (c), step (d)); `digests/master-data.md`; `docs/spec/SPEC-ibp-m1-master-data.md`; `db/schema.sql`; Open questions: Decided gate r13 (OQ-24, OQ-26 closed), gate r15, gate r16, critic r10, gate r17, gate r18, critic r11; Decided gate r19, OQ-35, OQ-36; Action U11; To-be T13; Requirements DF-13, NW-08.

### Phase 6 — Confirmations flow back (bridge and back-allocation)
- **Goal:** a confirmation at MK × plant moves the confirmed inventory row, the confirmed FMS and the gap at LE × region × PM at once; Excel back-allocation and dependent-demand files replaced; the bridge groups that the production alert, the FGNI mapping, the frozen-zone shares and the sales plant view need exist and are measured on history.
- **Delivers:** NW-01 (bridge links of every class on ITEM LINKS, weights, alias pins of the merged dEPM IDs, bridge groups), NW-02, NW-03 (plant-qualified semi-finished links incl. the `-BA` two-step; AC6 load part); confirmed side of the CP-02 switch and confirmed FMS (CP-03); FGNI mapping from plant × MK (CP-02); DF-09 history measurement; UX-05 plant / cluster view in the CLI (sourcing-aware plant shares, Assembly Cluster filter); **CP-09 value gap and DF-14 FX rates** (entry by file / CLI). **Cases:** B1-B12, P8 (load), N1 (confirmed), N3 (confirmed), N4 (plant × MK arrival), S2 (regional gap), S10 (CLI), E24 (history), N5, P12 (back-allocation per pair).
- **Exit check:** stored R-instance values since 06/2025 reproduced (verification), mismatches per key; a confirmation edit moves only the confirmed row and the confirmed FMS; confirmed FMS vs stored `FMSRate_conf_calc` (reference 98.7 % [fms]), mismatches listed; **YCRANE: 7 MK items, 24 PM items, 24 bridge links of 100 %**, back-allocated by request %; every PM's bridge weights sum to 100 % in every month, 1:N weights seeded at equal shares are listed; merged planning models' links collapsed onto the stem's link, missing bases listed with volume; back-allocation per MK × plant pair: a confirmation at a plant reaches only the cells whose request reached it (a LATAM-only plant confirms no EMEA cell); unmatched links listed; as-of guard respected. **`-BA` two-step [critic r10]:** for a paired `-BA` key, the `-BA` planning model's request loads its base MK at Niagara and books the `-BA` basic assembly at Northfield two months earlier, once; a load of the same base MK at Northfield books no basic assembly; no routing option carries a two-step; `-BA` PMs and items left unpaired by the Phase 5 counts are listed with volume. **New pair at Niagara [critic r11]:** for a paired `-BA` key whose base MK has no Niagara ITEM × PLANT row in Confirmed history, the pinned or sourced Niagara load is accepted and flagged "new key × plant pair" (AC5), counted and listed, and the semi-finished link still books the basic assembly at Northfield two months earlier. **AC6, load part [gate2r14]:** one finished request at Cerritos generates the semi-finished dependent demand at Ganderkesee two months earlier, read from the link's offset and qualified on Cerritos, and the finishing load at Cerritos; the same MK's load at any other plant generates none; both plants carry load at MK × plant × month — the rows the Phase 7 capacity nodes consume (the capacity part is asserted in Phase 7 on the same fixture, Plan "AC6 placement"; split confirmed [gate2r15]). **Plant / cluster view (UX-05):** a PM made at two plants (a P1 key, and the folded B5 item) is printed under each plant and each cluster with its share, and the plant rows plus "(unassigned plant)" sum to the cell in every month; a planning model sold in LATAM and APAC under different sourcing rules shows under the plants each region's rule reaches; the cluster filter groups by the planning model's Assembly Cluster, planning models without one under "(no cluster)". **Value gap:** a constructed 10-unit shortfall at an LE whose currency is not EUR gives the value in local currency and in EUR at the month's FX rate; a month without its own rate uses the latest, labelled; a currency with no rate gives no EUR value, flagged and counted; cross-LE totals are only in EUR; with no price entered in the price table every value cell is blank and counted. A plant × MK FGNI fixture is mapped to LE × region × PM with totals preserved and provenance "mapped". DF-09 on history, per bridge group × month: ops `C_Total` I (plant × MK, plants summed) vs sales-implied production = Δ inventory + External invoiced, every LE of the group summed. A constructed intra-group case (one LE invoices 10 units as Group to another LE that keeps them) raises no alert on External and would raise one on Total. Printed: group sizes, volume outside the alert (B9, B11, `-BA` semi-finished items), alert volume per month at ≥ 1 unit, the region breakdown and the cross-region volume of Own Region + Group invoices per group (decides whether the alert can move to region grain), the residuals that do not net inside one group — the in-transit residual *(inference)* and the **cross-group intra-group volume** (sender and receiver rows on different bridge groups) —, the Ops actuals region read as plant or destination, Δ inventory Financial + IC + FGNI vs Financial + IC vs Financial only, the External (default) vs Total invoiced variant, and the volume of "Dummy Sales Division" ops `Inventory` per month as candidate history of plant-held stock *(inference)*.
- **Read:** Cases B, N1, N3, N4, P8, S2, S10, E24; As-is A1, A1b, A1c, A2, A3, DS3, DS11, DD5, DD6, DD9, DD11, DM3, DM4, DM8; Requirements NW-01…NW-03, CP-01, CP-02, CP-03, CP-07, DF-08, DF-09, DF-12, UX-05; To-be T2.3, T2.7, T2.8, T2.14, T4, T6, T12; Plan ("AC6 placement", item-master refactor); Decided gate r13 (OQ-24 closed), gate r16, critic r10, gate r17, gate r18, critic r11; Decided gate r19, OQ-35, OQ-36; Requirements CP-09, DF-14, NW-08; To-be T13.

### Phase 7 — Capacity, allocation, the frozen zone and the Planning BOM
- **Goal:** while planners edit, the engine shows hours and production slots on every key and installation week-slots on CIT keys, shows the live allocation proposal across plants, takes the Operations Orderbook (the assembly plants' sales orders) from the ops planner, and splits each Requested cell into MK × plant shares so that only the shares inside their pair's frozen zone follow the order book, with manual changes of a frozen share as exceptions with a reason, and shows those shares to sales in three states; in a last wave, the Planning BOM's routing group splits routing MKs across plants once, its component groups explode the load at each plant into component requirements by rate, phantoms pass through, ordered units follow their real configuration, and the chain of a PM can be printed.
- **Delivers:** CP-01 capacity nodes and frozen-share default node, NW-05 (live measure, on the Operations Orderbook, allowed plants by priority, capacity-breach proposals on the next allowed plant), NW-06 (hours at plant × cluster, production slots per key, week-slots in calendar weeks), CP-08 (U4; zone lengths per MK × plant on ITEM × PLANT; fills the DF-10 frozen-zone hook; Requested shares split on the pre-default request, else the CP-07 split × link weights, the split stored per cell; default from the Operations Orderbook per MK × plant, allocation to LE × region × PM on the same basis, edit spread over open shares, exception with reason on a frozen share, UNASSIGNED handling), UX-05 frozen states in the CLI, SC-02 (Requested over open shares), DF-10 Operations Orderbook on channel 1 (no history exists [gate2r18]; pilot fixtures = a proxy from the ops cube `OrderBook_units`, a copy of the sales order book, labelled "proxy: sales order book" [AD], plus constructed late-order fixtures). Ops inputs (Working Days, Cluster average max capacity) via DF-10 channel 1 at their DF-10 grains; hour master data seeded. **Last wave — Planning BOM:** NW-07 with NW-04 inside it on ITEM LINKS (component items; routing group with options targeting ITEM × PLANT rows by plant ID and component groups, attachments, conditions on a routing option, dated rate sets per group MK × region and global; phantom-explosion links with the cycle check over overlapping validities; rate proposal from a test-double configuration history), the CP-07 routing-group tier, the ordered / remainder node fed by a **test-double configured sales Order Book** — rows at LE × region × PM × month with ordered units and, per order line, the chosen routing option and component options — until U1, the component-requirement node with phantom pass-through, master-data entry of the BOM on channel 1 (CLI / file until the Phase 12 mask), and the UX-04 chain print in the CLI. **Cases:** P1, P3, P8 (capacity), P9, P10, P11, B4, B5, C1-C3, V2, V8, V9, V10 (CLI), S3, PB1-PB9.
- **Exit check:** week-slots only on the CIT ITEM × PLANT rows (62); production-slot capacity = entered capacity × entered working days per plant × cluster [gate2r19], line-key rows excluded, and with history fixtures the per-key history summed to the cluster matches the A2b baseline; hours per plant × cluster vs the seeded plant × cluster hour capacity, stored `AssemblyHours` reproduced (verification); D-001 on Basic Assemblies CL3/CL4/CL5 (E): slot and hour exhaustion tested separately; line-key production shown as unassigned; allocator override traced. **AC6, capacity part [gate2r14]:** the Phase 6 Cerritos fixture rerun — hours and production slots are consumed at **both** Ganderkesee (semi-finished, two months earlier) and Cerritos (finishing); a capacity breach at either plant shows on that plant; AC6 closes here (split confirmed [gate2r15]). **Frozen zone (seeded zone list; checks that need no confirm or rebase):** the frozen months are marked in the masks and not locked; each Requested cell shows its MK × plant shares and their sum equals the cell. **Non-circular split:** a cell with a pre-default request keeps that split after the default lands, and a second order-book landing does not change the split; a cell never entered splits on the CP-07 split × link weights; no test path reads the defaulted load as a split basis. **Multi-plant test:** a planning model linked to the folded item `CIT-11-01-000` with ITEM × PLANT rows Oakridge and Northfield (B5), and a key producible at two plants (P1), with different zone lengths or Working Days per plant — the never-entered cells split by the items' default shares, not by the product `Plant` attribute [critic r10]; in a month frozen at one plant and open at the other, only the frozen pair's share equals the Operations Orderbook, the open share keeps the planner's value; an edit of the cell total moves only the open share; an edit of the frozen share in the breakdown, or of a cell whose shares are all frozen, opens S-17 and is rejected without a reason. **Sales display (UX-05, CLI):** the same cell prints "partially frozen, 1 of 2 plants" with per-plant sub-rows (share %, units, frozen months), each plant's zone end, and, under a plant filter, only that plant's share and state; a cell frozen at both plants prints "frozen". A P11 pair uses the supplying plant's zone. An UNASSIGNED share takes no default and the cell is listed for the agenda. In the common plan, an Operations Orderbook landing that moves an order to a later month moves its units in the frozen share, and inventory, FMS, load and gap follow (late-order test, V9); keyed rows set their LE × region × PM, others are allocated on the non-circular basis with totals preserved; an exception edit inside a scenario is logged with the default value and reason; a missing order book keeps the last default labelled "order book missing". **Sourcing in allocation:** a LATAM preferred plant whose capacity is breached gets a proposal moving the excess to the next allowed plant, applied only when accepted (traced); a sourcing-rule change dated inside a frozen pair's zone leaves that frozen share unchanged. Printed per bridge group: the proxy's totals vs the sales Order Book (equal by construction), the share of frozen units allocated rather than keyed, the count of cells split on each basis (pre-default request vs CP-07 split), and the count of cells with mixed frozen and open shares. Exception survival after a later landing, the agenda flag and the PR-06 alert need confirm and rebase and are checked in Phase 9. **Planning BOM (constructed fixtures; real content per U9):** saving an exclusive group whose rates do not sum to 100 % is rejected, an attach rate outside 0-100 % is rejected, a second routing group on one MK is rejected, a 0 % option rate is accepted, a routing option naming a plant ID that is not a plant is rejected and one naming a plant outside the MK's rows is accepted and flagged, a routing option with a two-step target is impossible by schema; **PB6 case [critic r9]:** a routing group red → plant X 30 %, blue → plant Y 70 %, 100 requested units, nothing ordered, gives 30 units at X and 70 at Y, and red declared as a component gives red = 30 at X and 0 at Y (never 9 at X and 21 at Y); a component group conditioned on the red option lands only at X, an unconditioned component group (Pump 1 / 2 / 3 at 40 / 20 / 40 %) yields 12 / 6 / 12 at X and 28 / 14 / 28 at Y, unrounded, each group total equal to the load at that plant; **two-step through routing [critic r10]:** a routing group Northfield 60 % / Niagara 40 % on an MK with a Niagara-qualified semi-finished link to its `-BA` item, 100 units, books 60 at Northfield, 40 at Niagara and 40 basic assemblies at Northfield two months earlier — never 100; **PB5 case [critic r9]:** 100 requested in one cell, 40 ordered red → Niagara in the test-double Order Book, red rate 30 % → Niagara 40 + 30 % × 60 = 58 units, the other plant 70 % × 60 = 42, and components follow the same ordered-then-remainder split; a cell with ordered 120 > requested 100 routes and explodes the 120 ordered units, remainder 0, and is flagged "ordered exceeds requested"; a cell with no ordered units in a month is outside its order-book horizon and goes by rates only; **PB9 phantom:** an MK whose component option at 40 % targets a phantom with children A (2 per phantom) and B (1 per phantom), 100 units at one plant, yields A = 80 and B = 40 at that plant and month, and no phantom row exists in the requirement, the load, the inventory or any mask; a phantom inside a phantom multiplies through; a phantom without children, a phantom as the target of a bridge, semi-finished or routing link, and a cycle of phantom links are rejected on save — including a cycle closed by two links whose validities overlap only in a future month [critic r10]; **regional fallback:** a region rate set for a group replaces the whole group for that region (all options), sums to 100 %, and a region without one takes the global group; the confirmed side uses the global rates; a rate row dated from month k changes the requirement from k on only; a Requested edit moves the plant split and the component requirement at once; an attachment at 30 % yields 30 units on 100 independently of the groups; **tier order on real data [critic r9; critic r10]:** on a real-data MK — a LC 10000 key (its caption is on 6 MK dimension rows [measured r981]), else the first P1 multi-plant key — a constructed routing group overrides the default shares, a rate change changes the split (AC7), a planner override and an alias pin still win over the routing group, the routing group wins over a sourcing rule (an option outside the region's allowed plants flagged "routing outside sourcing"), a sourcing rule wins over the default shares, and a multi-plant MK × region with neither splits on its default shares, never on the product `Plant` attribute; a plant reached only through a routing option and outside the MK's ITEM × PLANT rows is accepted and flagged (AC5); a semi-finished key with its own BOM explodes on its dependent demand at the offset month and no option is exploded further except through a phantom; a test-double configuration history produces a rate proposal, its acceptance and an override are logged with the proposed value; no component capacity node exists and the requirement is keyed component × plant × region × month; MKs with and without a Planning BOM are counted, and the MKs without a Planning BOM are listed; per routing MK the load by master split vs by routing group is printed (risk 16). **Chain print (UX-04):** for one PM the CLI prints PM → MK weights → plant (tier used, ordered vs remainder) → semi-finished (offset, finishing plant) → components (rate used, region group or global, condition; phantoms as pass-through steps) → overrides, and each figure equals the engine value.
- **Read:** Cases P, C, B4, B5, B7, S3, V8, V9, V10, PB; As-is A1, A1b, A2, A2b, A3, A7, DS3, DS5, DS7, DS8, DS9, DS10, DS11, DD10, DD11; Requirements NW-02…NW-07, CP-01, CP-07, CP-08, SC-02, DF-10, DF-11, DF-12, PR-02, UX-04, UX-05; To-be T2.4, T2.6, T2.12, T2.13, T2.14, T3, T4, T6, T12; Plan (frozen-zone checks placement, AC6 placement, Planning BOM placement, item-master refactor); `docs/spec/SPEC-ibp-m1-master-data.md` (the Planning BOM and the item master as realigned); Decided gate r13 (OQ-25 closed), gate r14, critic r9, gate r16, critic r10, gate r17, gate r18 (U10 closed); Decided gate r19, OQ-35, OQ-36; Actions U1, U4, U9; Requirements NW-08; To-be T13.

### Phase 8 — Scenario depth, visibility, confirm and scopes
- **Goal:** aggregate edits, nested scenarios, rebase, the three visibility levels and confirm as a commit into the common plan, visible at once to the other role; the engine side of access control: every read and write carries a principal and is filtered by role scope; M2-ready (T10).
- **Delivers:** SC-02, SC-03 (confirm), SC-05, SC-06, SC-07 (confirm), DF-03 (common plan node on the Phase 5 identity), UX-03 engine side (principal in the read and write path, read / write scopes, view-only enforcement, scope-aware roll-ups, users / roles / scopes / plant → region as master data per the DF-11 spec, plant → region seeded from the plant `Region` attribute with the 5 nulls visible only to `all` scopes, active role and role switch without sign-out [gate2r18 UX-Q-17], read scopes incl. Product Division [gate2r18 MD-Q-6], the global supply planner's and Operations' write rights (T11), test accounts for the nine roles of T11) [AD]. **Cases:** S1 (write scope), S2 (ops reads only confirmed Requested), S3-S7, B10 (region scope), P4-P6.
- **Exit check:** AC14-AC16 green, AC13 as rewritten in Phase 5; a personal scenario is invisible to every other principal; a shared scenario is read-only; the ops principal's Requested shows only values confirmed into the common plan; confirm only by an approver from review or by the data owner, approver recorded; confirm is rejected until rebase when the common plan changed meanwhile, and rebase reports vanished cells; after confirm, the other role's principal reads the new values on its next read; provenance for every confirmed row; no write to the common plan bypasses confirm except landed facts. **Scope leak test:** for each T11 role, every read (rows, compare, KPIs, alerts, FMS and gap roll-ups, the UX-05 plant view) returns only in-scope rows and in-scope sums; a Regional Head never sees rows of a plant without region; view-only principals are rejected on every write; a planner's write outside its LE / plant is rejected (FGNI and Operations Orderbook outside the ops planner's plants included); a call without a principal is rejected; a grant restricted to one Product Division sees only rows of that division (ops rows through the bridged planning models) and no row without a division; a user holding two roles switches without signing out and every read and write after the switch follows the new role's scopes, the log naming the active role; the global supply planner can edit ITEM × PLANT rows, default shares and sourcing rules and cannot write sales figures or Confirmed production; an ops planner can edit hour capacity, AAH, week-slot parameters and Planning BOM rates only for its plants.
- **Read:** Cases S, B10, P4-P6; As-is A3, A6, DC5-DC10; Requirements SC, DF-03, DF-04, DF-11, DF-12, UX-03, UX-05, RG-01; To-be T2.1, T2.9, T2.10, T4, T5, T7, T10, T11; Plan (identity refactor, access control placement); `digests/master-data.md`; `docs/spec/SPEC-ibp-m1-master-data.md` (users, roles, scopes); Decided gate r18; Decided gate r19, OQ-35, OQ-36.

### Phase 9 — The continuous cycle and the monthly review
- **Goal:** one month replayed on the continuous common plan: m−1 actuals and openings land; changes confirmed by the sales role raise alerts for the ops role and the reverse; Operations Orderbook landings move the frozen-share defaults; the monthly review sees alerts, gaps, missing openings, unreported FGNI, frozen-zone exceptions, UNASSIGNED load and plants without region, decides, publishes `CYCLE MM YYYY R` and `C`, hands them to dEPM by file, freezes the cycle node and opens the next cycle with its shortened (or, in January, extended) horizon.
- **Delivers:** PR-01…PR-06 (AC20-AC23; PR-03 fills the DF-10 lock hook; PR-04 with new-model bundles), SC-07 (publish, supersede, handoff under the dEPM aliases), RG-01 (AC13 publish test), DF-09 (live, on the Phase 6 bridge groups), DF-10 channels 2-3 (adapter interface + test doubles, Operations Orderbook and FGNI included), the CP-08 checks that need confirm and rebase. **Cases:** S2, S6-S8, V1-V3, V5, V8, V9, N4, B10, P3, E13, E24, S11.
- **Exit check:** one month replayed end-to-end with history fixtures landed through the input layer, with no day gate in the tool. A Requested change confirmed by the sales principal shows at once to the ops principal as load, gap and alert; a confirmation by the ops principal shows at once to the sales principal as confirmed inventory, FMS and shortfall alert; a capacity breach alerts ops; an Operations Orderbook landing that moves a frozen-share default alerts sales; **frozen-zone exceptions:** an exception made in a scenario survives a later Operations Orderbook landing through rebase, and once confirmed it appears on the agenda with its reason and raises an alert to ops; view-only principals see only alerts inside their scope. Alerts carry the causing change, author and cells; their volume per month is printed and the thresholds are set from it. Actuals landing triggers rebase; missing openings and unreported FGNI are flagged. DF-09 alerts are raised per bridge group × month on External depletion with the PM, MK and region breakdown, and approval is logged. A test-double ERP extract and a test-double Snowflake source land the same actuals, openings, FGNI and Operations Orderbook as channel 1, with identical validation, log, provenance and results; a second order-book source (test double) plugs in. A sourcing-rule change by the global supply planner that moves requested load alerts ops. **Portfolio Review:** a proposed new-model bundle (PM, MK, ITEM × REGION with SOP, bridge, ITEM × PLANT, sourcing) is invisible to the engine until approved; once approved, the PM's cells appear for its regions from SOP on and its load reaches the plants of its rule; a rejected bundle stays logged. The monthly review lists alerts, gaps, missing openings, unreported FGNI, frozen-zone exceptions, UNASSIGNED load, plants without region, sourcing flags, bundles and value-gap cells without price or FX; then the approver publishes R and C with their own identity beside the loaded references. A re-publish supersedes; the handoff file carries R and C (P / A copies if configured) under the dEPM IDs of the aliases (merged planning models by the alias split), and its month count is printed for a December and a January replay; writes on the closed node are rejected; the next cycle opens from the published versions with the CP-01 horizon; no published version reaches Power BI.
- **Read:** As-is A1, A1b, DS1-DS8, DD9; Requirements PR, CP-01, CP-02, CP-08, SC-05…SC-07, DF-03, DF-09, DF-10, NW-01, NW-02, NW-06, UX-03, RG-01; To-be T2.1, T2.8, T2.9, T2.11, T2.12, T3 (incl. "Channels in M1"), T7, T10, T11, T12 (handoff); Plan (frozen-zone checks placement); Decided gate r13 (OQ-24…OQ-26 closed), gate r15; Actions U1, U5, U6, U11; Requirements PR-04, NW-08.

### Phase 10 — Accuracy control I: the judge (off the critical path; history only)
- **Goal:** one harness scores humans and tool on one universe of DWH history.
- **Delivers:** QC-01, QC-02, QC-03, QC-08. **Cases:** E1-E4, E13-E15, E17, E22, E24, K1-K4.
- **Exit check (reported):** `baseline_v2` headline reproduced to ±0.01 pp on the unmerged dEPM IDs, the merged-alias view reported beside it (QC-01); human table on U; Requested production vs ops I and vs sales I.
- **Read:** Cases E, K; As-is A4, A5, DP, DK, DD1, DD8, DD9; Requirements QC-01…QC-03, QC-08, DF-08; To-be T8.

### Phase 11 — Accuracy control II: statistical forecast (off the critical path)
- **Goal:** STAT trained on history at LN × PM, unbiased, pre-fills the demand layer as a scenario to the end of the next FY; ops forecasts; accuracy report.
- **Delivers:** QC-04…QC-07 (QC-07 STAT → R on replayed cycles), QC-09; OpenMP SIGSEGV import rule [project]. **Cases:** E11-E13, E16, E21, V1.
- **Exit check (reported):** QC-05 table on U at lags 3-6, per-LN wins, sign test; QC-06 for both ops routes; QC-07 FVA on replayed cycles.
- **Read:** As-is A5, DP1, DP2, DP5, DP6; Requirements QC, CP-01, DF-08; To-be T8.

### Phase 12 — Windows app, login, server, API, master-data masks, chain view, diagrams
- **Goal:** users work without the CLI in the Windows desktop app: they log in, and each role sees its views and scope; the app shows input masks per role over the two-FY horizon with current-FY actuals and an "i" definition on every input field — including the opening-inventory mask (S-06) with its Financial / IC / FGNI info pop-up, the sales mask (S-04) with the Order Intake forecast, the plant and cluster filters and the per-plant frozen-zone display, the ops mask with the Operations Orderbook and the latest confirmed Requested (S-07), and the frozen-zone exception dialog (S-17) —, the alert inbox, the scenario tree with visibility level, an edit grid with inventory switch, consequence and compare panels, the monthly review view with the cockpit agenda, the chain read view (UX-04), and the master-data maintenance masks laid out per dimension as the DF-11 spec defines, the item master (default plant shares, sourcing rules and semi-finished link qualifiers included), the calendar, the FX rates, the Order Intake level, the new-model bundles and the Planning BOM included; the S-06 pop-up opens once per cycle; the header carries the role switch; English interface; an accuracy view is added if Phase 11 is done. The tool server process (engine + API-01) serves many authenticated readers and serializes writes.
- **Delivers:** UX-01 (incl. the "i" texts written by the UX designer from the vault's S&OP documentation; the Financial / IC / FGNI texts and the S-06 pop-up state the decided rule — a plant-held unit is entered only as FGNI, never in IC [gate2r13]; the Total texts state that Total is typed and is not depletion), UX-02 (AC25, AC26), UX-03 platform side (login with tool-local accounts [gate2r18 U7], role switch without sign-out, sessions, server process owning the store, scopes enforced in the API before any value reaches a client), UX-04 (chain read view in the app, scoped, each step linked to its maintenance mask), UX-05 (S-04 plant / cluster filters and the three-state frozen display), DF-11 maintenance layouts (item master and Planning BOM included). **Cases:** S7 (scoped viewers), S9 (mask), S10 (S-04), B10, V8 (zone and shares shown in the masks), V10 (S-04), N1, N4 (S-06), PB1-PB9 (maintenance and chain view).
- **Exit check:** done in the Windows app, on pilot fixtures: the Phase 5 flow, one Phase 8 confirm seen across two logged-in sessions, one frozen-share exception entered through S-17, and one Phase 9 monthly review. Every input field of the five masks shows its "i" definition, and S-06 opens its Financial / IC / FGNI pop-up. **S-04:** filtered by plant, a PM made at two plants shows only that plant's share, and the shares under both plants plus "(unassigned plant)" add up to the unfiltered cell; sorted by cluster, rows group by the planning model's Assembly Cluster, a planning model without one under "(no cluster)" [gate2r17 OQ-29; critic r12]; a cell frozen at one of two plants shows "1 of 2 plants", expands into its plant sub-rows with share %, units and frozen months, shows a tooltip per plant and each plant's zone end in the header band; an edit of the total moves only the open share, and an edit of an all-frozen cell or of a frozen sub-row opens S-17. The chain view of one PM shows the same figures as the Phase 7 CLI print and only in-scope rows for a scoped login. Each dimension and structure of the master-data spec can be maintained through its layout by the data manager, the item master and the Planning BOM included (a group not summing to 100 %, a second routing group on one MK, a phantom cycle, default plant shares not summing to 100 % on a multi-plant item, sourcing shares not summing to 100 % and two preferred plants are refused on save); each master-data mask accepts writes only from the roles of T11 (the global supply planner on ITEM × PLANT and sourcing, Operations on capacity parameters and rates of its plants, the data manager on all). A user switches role in the header without signing out. S-06 opens its pop-up on the first visit of each cycle only. Readers in parallel: a Manager, a Regional Head and a Corporate login read at the same time as a planner writes, and the scope leak test of Phase 8 is rerun through the API with no out-of-scope row reaching a client. The app refuses access without login. Single-PC mode runs offline. The user picks the diagrams.
- **Read:** Requirements UX (incl. UX-04, UX-05), DF-10, DF-11, DF-12, CP-02, CP-07, CP-08, NW-03, NW-07, PR-05, PR-06; To-be T2.10, T2.11, T2.12, T2.13, T2.14, T3, T5, T7, T9, T10, T11, T12; As-is A6, A7; Plan (access control placement); `docs/spec/SPEC-ibp-m1-master-data.md`; Actions U7, U9, U11; Decided gate r13, gate r14, critic r9, gate r16, critic r10, gate r17, gate r18; Open questions OQ-35, OQ-36; Decided gate r19; Requirements CP-09, DF-13, DF-14, NW-08, PR-04; Phase 5, Phase 7 and Phase 8 briefs.
